// ---- ps-runtime.js ---- /* TerminalEX — PowerShell runtime: values, operators, formatting, evaluation */ (function () { 'use strict'; const TX = window.TX; const C = TX.C; // ============================================================ value model class PSHash { constructor(ordered) { this.m = new Map(); this.ordered = !!ordered; } get(k) { const e = this.m.get(String(k).toLowerCase()); return e ? e[1] : undefined; } set(k, v) { const lk = String(k).toLowerCase(); const e = this.m.get(lk); if (e) e[1] = v; else this.m.set(lk, [k, v]); } has(k) { return this.m.has(String(k).toLowerCase()); } del(k) { this.m.delete(String(k).toLowerCase()); } keys() { return [...this.m.values()].map((e) => e[0]); } values() { return [...this.m.values()].map((e) => e[1]); } entries() { return [...this.m.values()]; } get size() { return this.m.size; } } class PSScriptBlock { constructor(node, rt) { this.node = node; this.rt = rt; } toString() { return this.node.text || ''; } } class PSError extends Error { constructor(msg, opts = {}) { super(msg); this.ps = true; Object.assign(this, opts); } } class Sig { constructor(kind, value) { this.kind = kind; this.value = value; } } TX.PSHash = PSHash; TX.PSScriptBlock = PSScriptBlock; TX.PSError = PSError; const isObj = (v) => v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) && !(v instanceof Date) && !(v instanceof PSHash) && !(v instanceof PSScriptBlock) && !(v instanceof RegExp); const visibleKeys = (o) => Object.keys(o).filter((k) => !k.startsWith('__')); TX.psVisibleKeys = visibleKeys; function typeName(v) { if (v === null || v === undefined) return 'null'; if (typeof v === 'string') return 'System.String'; if (typeof v === 'number') return Number.isInteger(v) && Math.abs(v) < 2147483648 ? 'System.Int32' : Number.isInteger(v) ? 'System.Int64' : 'System.Double'; if (typeof v === 'boolean') return 'System.Boolean'; if (Array.isArray(v)) return 'System.Object[]'; if (v instanceof Date) return 'System.DateTime'; if (v instanceof PSHash) return v.ordered ? 'System.Collections.Specialized.OrderedDictionary' : 'System.Collections.Hashtable'; if (v instanceof PSScriptBlock) return 'System.Management.Automation.ScriptBlock'; if (v.__type) return v.__type; return 'System.Management.Automation.PSCustomObject'; } TX.psTypeName = typeName; function numStr(n) { if (!isFinite(n)) return isNaN(n) ? 'NaN' : n > 0 ? '∞' : '-∞'; if (Number.isInteger(n)) return String(n); let s = String(Number(n.toPrecision(15))); if (/e/.test(s)) s = n.toExponential(14).replace(/\.?0+e/, 'E').replace('E+', 'E+').replace(/E(-?)(\d)$/, 'E$10$2'); return s; } function dateStr(d) { return TX.p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + '/' + TX.p2(d.getDate()) + '/' + d.getFullYear() + ' ' + TX.p2(d.getHours()) + ':' + TX.p2(d.getMinutes()) + ':' + TX.p2(d.getSeconds()); } function toStr(v, rt) { if (v === null || v === undefined) return ''; if (typeof v === 'string') return v; if (typeof v === 'number') return numStr(v); if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v ? 'True' : 'False'; if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map((x) => toStr(x, rt)).join(rt && rt.getVar('OFS') != null ? toStr(rt.getVar('OFS')) : ' '); if (v instanceof Date) return dateStr(v); if (v instanceof PSHash) return typeName(v); if (v instanceof PSScriptBlock) return v.toString(); if (v.__toString) return v.__toString(); if (v.__type === 'System.IO.FileInfo' || v.__type === 'System.IO.DirectoryInfo') return v.FullName; if (v.__type === 'System.Diagnostics.Process') return `System.Diagnostics.Process (${v.ProcessName})`; if (v.__type === 'System.TimeSpan') return tsStr(v); if (v.__type && v.__type !== 'System.Management.Automation.PSCustomObject') return v.__type; return '@{' + visibleKeys(v).map((k) => `${k}=${toStr(v[k], rt)}`).join('; ') + '}'; } function tsStr(t) { const neg = t.Ticks < 0; const a = Math.abs(t.TotalMilliseconds); const d = Math.floor(a / 86400000), h = Math.floor(a / 3600000) % 24, m = Math.floor(a / 60000) % 60, s = Math.floor(a / 1000) % 60, ms = Math.round(a % 1000); return (neg ? '-' : '') + (d ? d + '.' : '') + TX.p2(h) + ':' + TX.p2(m) + ':' + TX.p2(s) + (ms ? '.' + String(ms).padStart(3, '0') + '0000' : ''); } TX.psStr = toStr; TX.psNumStr = numStr; function timespan(ms) { const o = { __type: 'System.TimeSpan', Ticks: ms * 10000, Days: Math.trunc(ms / 86400000), Hours: Math.trunc(ms / 3600000) % 24, Minutes: Math.trunc(ms / 60000) % 60, Seconds: Math.trunc(ms / 1000) % 60, Milliseconds: Math.round(ms % 1000), TotalDays: ms / 86400000, TotalHours: ms / 3600000, TotalMinutes: ms / 60000, TotalSeconds: ms / 1000, TotalMilliseconds: ms }; return o; } TX.psTimeSpan = timespan; function toNum(v, strict) { if (typeof v === 'number') return v; if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v ? 1 : 0; if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') return 0; if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length === 1) return toNum(v[0], strict); if (v instanceof Date) return v.getTime(); const s = String(v).trim(); const m = /^(-?)(0x[0-9a-f]+|\d*\.?\d+(?:e[+-]?\d+)?)(kb|mb|gb|tb|pb)?$/i.exec(s.replace(/,/g, '')); if (m) { let n = /^0x/i.test(m[2]) ? parseInt(m[2], 16) : parseFloat(m[2]); if (m[3]) n *= Math.pow(1024, ['kb', 'mb', 'gb', 'tb', 'pb'].indexOf(m[3].toLowerCase()) + 1); return m[1] ? -n : n; } if (strict) throw new PSError(`Cannot convert value "${toStr(v)}" to type "System.Int32". Error: "The input string '${toStr(v)}' was not in a correct format."`, { category: 'InvalidArgument', id: 'InvalidCastFromStringToInteger' }); return NaN; } TX.psNum = toNum; function truthy(v) { if (v === null || v === undefined) return false; if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v; if (typeof v === 'number') return v !== 0; if (typeof v === 'string') return v.length > 0; if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.length === 0 ? false : v.length === 1 ? truthy(v[0]) : true; return true; } TX.psTruthy = truthy; const unroll = (v) => (v === null || v === undefined ? [] : Array.isArray(v) ? v : [v]); const collapse = (arr) => (arr.length === 0 ? null : arr.length === 1 ? arr[0] : arr); TX.psCollapse = collapse; // ------------------------------------------------------------ .NET-style format strings function fmtNumber(v, spec) { const n = toNum(v); if (!spec) return toStr(v); const m = /^([NnFfCcPpDdXxEeGg])(\d*)$/.exec(spec); if (m) { const p = m[2] === '' ? null : parseInt(m[2], 10); switch (m[1].toUpperCase()) { case 'N': return n.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: p ?? 2, maximumFractionDigits: p ?? 2 }); case 'F': return n.toFixed(p ?? 2); case 'C': return (n < 0 ? '-' : '') + '$' + Math.abs(n).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: p ?? 2, maximumFractionDigits: p ?? 2 }); case 'P': return (n * 100).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: p ?? 2, maximumFractionDigits: p ?? 2 }) + '%'; case 'D': return (n < 0 ? '-' : '') + String(Math.abs(Math.trunc(n))).padStart(p || 0, '0'); case 'X': { const s = (Math.trunc(n) >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(p || 0, '0'); return m[1] === 'X' ? s.toUpperCase() : s; } case 'E': return n.toExponential(p ?? 6).replace(/e([+-])(\d)$/, 'E$1' + '00$2').replace(/e([+-])(\d\d)$/, 'E$10$2'); case 'G': return numStr(n); } } // custom: 0.00, #,##0.0, 00000 if (/^[#0,.%]+$/.test(spec)) { const pct = spec.includes('%'); const val = pct ? n * 100 : n; const [ip, fp = ''] = spec.replace('%', '').split('.'); const minFrac = (fp.match(/0/g) || []).length, maxFrac = fp.length; const minInt = (ip.replace(/,/g, '').match(/0/g) || []).length; let s = Math.abs(val).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: minFrac, maximumFractionDigits: maxFrac, useGrouping: ip.includes(',') }); const [si, sf] = s.split('.'); const padded = si.replace(/,/g, '').length < minInt ? si.padStart(minInt, '0') : si; s = padded + (sf !== undefined ? '.' + sf : ''); return (val < 0 ? '-' : '') + s + (pct ? '%' : ''); } return toStr(v); } function fmtDate(d, spec) { if (!(d instanceof Date)) d = new Date(d); const std = { d: 'M/d/yyyy', D: 'dddd, MMMM d, yyyy', t: 'h:mm tt', T: 'h:mm:ss tt', g: 'M/d/yyyy h:mm tt', G: 'M/d/yyyy h:mm:ss tt', f: 'dddd, MMMM d, yyyy h:mm tt', F: 'dddd, MMMM d, yyyy h:mm:ss tt', s: "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss", u: "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'Z'", o: "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss.fffffffK", M: 'MMMM d', m: 'MMMM d', Y: 'MMMM yyyy', y: 'MMMM yyyy' }; if (!spec) spec = 'G'; if (std[spec]) spec = std[spec]; let out = ''; const re = /(dddd|ddd|dd|d|MMMM|MMM|MM|M|yyyy|yy|HH|H|hh|h|mm|m|ss|s|fffffff|fff|ff|f|tt|t|zzz|zz|K|'[^']*'|\\.|.)/g; let m; const h12 = d.getHours() % 12 || 12; while ((m = re.exec(spec))) { const k = m[1]; switch (k) { case 'dddd': out += TX.DAYS[d.getDay()]; break; case 'ddd': out += TX.DAY[d.getDay()]; break; case 'dd': out += TX.p2(d.getDate()); break; case 'd': out += d.getDate(); break; case 'MMMM': out += TX.MONTHS[d.getMonth()]; break; case 'MMM': out += TX.MON[d.getMonth()]; break; case 'MM': out += TX.p2(d.getMonth() + 1); break; case 'M': out += d.getMonth() + 1; break; case 'yyyy': out += d.getFullYear(); break; case 'yy': out += String(d.getFullYear()).slice(2); break; case 'HH': out += TX.p2(d.getHours()); break; case 'H': out += d.getHours(); break; case 'hh': out += TX.p2(h12); break; case 'h': out += h12; break; case 'mm': out += TX.p2(d.getMinutes()); break; case 'm': out += d.getMinutes(); break; case 'ss': out += TX.p2(d.getSeconds()); break; case 's': out += d.getSeconds(); break; case 'fffffff': out += String(d.getMilliseconds()).padStart(3, '0') + '0000'; break; case 'fff': out += String(d.getMilliseconds()).padStart(3, '0'); break; case 'ff': out += String(Math.floor(d.getMilliseconds() / 10)).padStart(2, '0'); break; case 'f': out += Math.floor(d.getMilliseconds() / 100); break; case 'tt': out += d.getHours() >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM'; break; case 't': out += d.getHours() >= 12 ? 'P' : 'A'; break; case 'zzz': case 'K': out += '-04:00'; break; case 'zz': out += '-04'; break; default: out += k.startsWith("'") ? k.slice(1, -1) : k.startsWith('\\') ? k[1] : k; } } return out; } TX.psFmtDate = fmtDate; function formatOp(fmt, args) { args = unroll(args); return String(fmt).replace(/\{\{|\}\}|\{(\d+)(?:,(-?\d+))?(?::([^}]*))?\}/g, (all, i, w, spec) => { if (all === '{{') return '{'; if (all === '}}') return '}'; const v = args[+i]; let s; if (v instanceof Date) s = fmtDate(v, spec); else if (spec && (typeof v === 'number' || /^[\d.-]+$/.test(String(v)))) s = fmtNumber(v, spec); else s = toStr(v); if (w) { const n = parseInt(w, 10); s = n < 0 ? s.padEnd(-n) : s.padStart(n); } return s; }); } TX.psFormat = formatOp; // ------------------------------------------------------------ member access function findKey(o, name) { const ln = String(name).toLowerCase(); return Object.keys(o).find((k) => k.toLowerCase() === ln); } function getMember(v, name, rt) { const ln = String(name).toLowerCase(); if (v === null || v === undefined) return null; if (typeof v === 'string') { if (ln === 'length') return v.length; return undefined; } if (Array.isArray(v)) { if (ln === 'count' || ln === 'length') return v.length; if (ln === 'rank') return 1; // member enumeration const out = []; v.forEach((x) => { const r = getMember(x, name, rt); if (r !== undefined && r !== null) out.push(...(Array.isArray(r) ? r : [r])); }); return collapse(out); } if (v instanceof Date) { const map = { year: v.getFullYear(), month: v.getMonth() + 1, day: v.getDate(), hour: v.getHours(), minute: v.getMinutes(), second: v.getSeconds(), millisecond: v.getMilliseconds(), dayofweek: TX.DAYS[v.getDay()], dayofyear: Math.floor((v - new Date(v.getFullYear(), 0, 0)) / 86400000), date: new Date(v.getFullYear(), v.getMonth(), v.getDate()), ticks: v.getTime() * 10000 + 621355968000000000, timeofday: timespan(v - new Date(v.getFullYear(), v.getMonth(), v.getDate())), kind: 'Local' }; return map[ln]; } if (v instanceof PSHash) { if (ln === 'keys') return v.keys(); if (ln === 'values') return v.values(); if (ln === 'count') return v.size; return v.has(name) ? v.get(name) : null; } if (typeof v === 'number') return undefined; if (v instanceof PSScriptBlock) return undefined; if (typeof v === 'object') { const k = findKey(v, name); if (k !== undefined && !k.startsWith('__')) { const val = v[k]; return typeof val === 'function' ? val.call(v) : val; } if (v.__getters && v.__getters[ln]) return v.__getters[ln](v); return null; } return undefined; } TX.psGetMember = getMember; function setMember(v, name, val) { if (v instanceof PSHash) { v.set(name, val); return; } if (v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v)) { const k = findKey(v, name); if (k === undefined) throw new PSError(`The property '${name}' cannot be found on this object. Verify that the property exists and can be set.`, { id: 'PropertyNotFound' }); v[k] = val; if (v.__onSet) v.__onSet(k, val); return; } throw new PSError(`The property '${name}' cannot be found on this object. Verify that the property exists and can be set.`, { id: 'PropertyNotFound' }); } function callMethod(v, name, args, rt) { const ln = String(name).toLowerCase(); const a = args; if (v === null || v === undefined) throw new PSError('You cannot call a method on a null-valued expression.', { id: 'InvokeMethodOnNull', category: 'InvalidOperation' }); if (ln === 'tostring') { if (v instanceof Date) return a.length ? fmtDate(v, toStr(a[0])) : dateStr(v); if (typeof v === 'number' && a.length) return fmtNumber(v, toStr(a[0])); return toStr(v, rt); } if (ln === 'gettype') return { __type: 'System.RuntimeType', IsPublic: true, IsSerial: true, Name: typeName(v).split('.').pop(), BaseType: typeName(v).startsWith('System.Int') || typeName(v) === 'System.Double' ? 'System.ValueType' : 'System.Object', FullName: typeName(v), __toString() { return typeName(v); } }; if (ln === 'equals') return toStr(v).toLowerCase() === toStr(a[0]).toLowerCase(); if (ln === 'gethashcode') return Math.abs(parseInt(TX.sha1(toStr(v)).slice(0, 7), 16)); if (typeof v === 'string') { const s = v; switch (ln) { case 'toupper': case 'toupperinvariant': return s.toUpperCase(); case 'tolower': case 'tolowerinvariant': return s.toLowerCase(); case 'trim': return a.length ? trimChars(s, toStr(a[0]), 3) : s.trim(); case 'trimstart': return a.length ? trimChars(s, toStr(a[0]), 1) : s.replace(/^\s+/, ''); case 'trimend': return a.length ? trimChars(s, toStr(a[0]), 2) : s.replace(/\s+$/, ''); case 'split': { if (!a.length) return s.split(/\s/); const seps = unroll(a[0]).map((x) => toStr(x)); const chars = seps.length === 1 && seps[0].length > 1 && typeof a[0] === 'string' ? [...seps[0]] : seps; return s.split(new RegExp(chars.map((c) => c.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')).join('|'))); } case 'replace': return s.split(toStr(a[0])).join(toStr(a[1])); case 'contains': return s.includes(toStr(a[0])); case 'startswith': return a[1] && /ignore/i.test(toStr(a[1])) ? s.toLowerCase().startsWith(toStr(a[0]).toLowerCase()) : s.startsWith(toStr(a[0])); case 'endswith': return a[1] && /ignore/i.test(toStr(a[1])) ? s.toLowerCase().endsWith(toStr(a[0]).toLowerCase()) : s.endsWith(toStr(a[0])); case 'indexof': return s.indexOf(toStr(a[0]), a[1] !== undefined ? toNum(a[1]) : 0); case 'lastindexof': return s.lastIndexOf(toStr(a[0])); case 'substring': { const st = toNum(a[0]); if (st > s.length) throw new PSError('Exception calling "Substring" with "1" argument(s): "startIndex cannot be larger than length of string. (Parameter \'startIndex\')"'); return a.length > 1 ? s.substr(st, toNum(a[1])) : s.slice(st); } case 'padleft': return s.padStart(toNum(a[0]), a[1] !== undefined ? toStr(a[1]) : ' '); case 'padright': return s.padEnd(toNum(a[0]), a[1] !== undefined ? toStr(a[1]) : ' '); case 'insert': return s.slice(0, toNum(a[0])) + toStr(a[1]) + s.slice(toNum(a[0])); case 'remove': return a.length > 1 ? s.slice(0, toNum(a[0])) + s.slice(toNum(a[0]) + toNum(a[1])) : s.slice(0, toNum(a[0])); case 'tochararray': return [...s]; case 'normalize': return s.normalize(); case 'compareto': return s.localeCompare(toStr(a[0])); } } if (typeof v === 'number') { if (ln === 'compareto') return Math.sign(v - toNum(a[0])); } if (Array.isArray(v)) { switch (ln) { case 'contains': return v.some((x) => looseEq(x, a[0])); case 'indexof': return v.findIndex((x) => looseEq(x, a[0])); case 'where': { const sb = a[0]; return v.filter((x) => truthy(rt.invokeBlock(sb, [], x))); } case 'foreach': { const sb = a[0]; if (sb instanceof PSScriptBlock) return v.map((x) => collapse(unroll(rt.invokeBlock(sb, [], x)))); return v.map((x) => getMember(x, toStr(sb), rt)); } case 'add': throw new PSError('Exception calling "Add" with "1" argument(s): "Collection was of a fixed size."'); case 'clone': return v.slice(); } } if (v instanceof Date) { const add = (ms) => new Date(v.getTime() + ms); switch (ln) { case 'adddays': return add(toNum(a[0]) * 86400000); case 'addhours': return add(toNum(a[0]) * 3600000); case 'addminutes': return add(toNum(a[0]) * 60000); case 'addseconds': return add(toNum(a[0]) * 1000); case 'addmilliseconds': return add(toNum(a[0])); case 'addmonths': { const d = new Date(v); d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + toNum(a[0])); return d; } case 'addyears': { const d = new Date(v); d.setFullYear(d.getFullYear() + toNum(a[0])); return d; } case 'toshortdatestring': return fmtDate(v, 'd'); case 'tolongdatestring': return fmtDate(v, 'D'); case 'toshorttimestring': return fmtDate(v, 't'); case 'tolongtimestring': return fmtDate(v, 'T'); case 'touniversaltime': return add(4 * 3600000); case 'tolocaltime': return v; case 'subtract': return a[0] instanceof Date ? timespan(v - a[0]) : add(-toNum(getMember(a[0], 'TotalMilliseconds'))); case 'isdaylightsavingtime': return true; } } if (v instanceof PSHash) { switch (ln) { case 'add': if (v.has(a[0])) throw new PSError(`Exception calling "Add" with "2" argument(s): "Item has already been added. Key in dictionary: '${toStr(a[0])}' Key being added: '${toStr(a[0])}'"`); v.set(a[0], a[1]); return undefined; case 'remove': v.del(a[0]); return undefined; case 'containskey': return v.has(a[0]); case 'containsvalue': return v.values().some((x) => looseEq(x, a[0])); case 'clear': v.m.clear(); return undefined; case 'getenumerator': return v.entries().map(([k, val]) => ({ __type: 'System.Collections.DictionaryEntry', Name: k, Key: k, Value: val })); case 'clone': { const h = new PSHash(v.ordered); v.entries().forEach(([k, val]) => h.set(k, val)); return h; } } } if (v instanceof PSScriptBlock && ln === 'invoke') return collapse(unroll(rt.invokeBlock(v, a))); if (v && typeof v === 'object' && v.__methods) { const k = Object.keys(v.__methods).find((x) => x.toLowerCase() === ln); if (k) return v.__methods[k].apply(v, a); } throw new PSError(`Method invocation failed because [${typeName(v)}] does not contain a method named '${name}'.`, { id: 'MethodNotFound', category: 'InvalidOperation' }); } function trimChars(s, chars, mode) { const set = new Set([...chars]); let a = 0, b = s.length; if (mode & 1) while (a < b && set.has(s[a])) a++; if (mode & 2) while (b > a && set.has(s[b - 1])) b--; return s.slice(a, b); } TX.psCallMethod = callMethod; TX.psSetMember = setMember; // ------------------------------------------------------------ operators function looseEq(a, b, cs) { if (a === null || a === undefined) return b === null || b === undefined; if (b === null || b === undefined) return false; if (typeof a === 'number') { const nb = typeof b === 'string' ? toNum(b) : toNum(b); return a === nb; } if (typeof a === 'boolean') return a === truthy(b); if (a instanceof Date) return b instanceof Date ? a.getTime() === b.getTime() : false; if (typeof a === 'string') { const sb = toStr(b); return cs ? a === sb : a.toLowerCase() === sb.toLowerCase(); } return a === b; } function compare(a, b, cs) { if (typeof a === 'number' || typeof b === 'number' && (a === null || /^\s*-?[\d.]+\s*$/.test(String(a)))) { const x = toNum(a), y = toNum(b); return x < y ? -1 : x > y ? 1 : 0; } if (a instanceof Date) { const y = b instanceof Date ? b.getTime() : new Date(toStr(b)).getTime(); return a.getTime() - y; } const x = cs ? toStr(a) : toStr(a).toLowerCase(), y = cs ? toStr(b) : toStr(b).toLowerCase(); return x < y ? -1 : x > y ? 1 : 0; } TX.psCompare = compare; TX.psLooseEq = looseEq; function binaryOp(op, l, r, rt) { let cs = false; let o = op; if (/^c(eq|ne|gt|ge|lt|le|like|notlike|match|notmatch|contains|notcontains|in|notin|replace|split)$/.test(o)) { cs = true; o = o.slice(1); } else if (/^i(eq|ne|gt|ge|lt|le|like|notlike|match|notmatch|contains|notcontains|in|notin|replace|split)$/.test(o)) o = o.slice(1); const scalarCmp = (fn) => { if (Array.isArray(l)) return l.filter((x) => fn(x)); return fn(l); }; switch (o) { case '+': if (l === null || l === undefined) return r; if (Array.isArray(l)) return l.concat(Array.isArray(r) ? r : [r]); if (typeof l === 'string') return l + toStr(r, rt); if (l instanceof PSHash) { const h = new PSHash(l.ordered); l.entries().forEach(([k, v]) => h.set(k, v)); if (r instanceof PSHash) r.entries().forEach(([k, v]) => { if (h.has(k)) throw new PSError(`Item has already been added. Key in dictionary: '${k}' Key being added: '${k}'`); h.set(k, v); }); return h; } if (l instanceof Date) return new Date(l.getTime() + (r && r.__type === 'System.TimeSpan' ? r.TotalMilliseconds : toNum(r) / 10000)); return toNum(l, true) + toNum(r, true); case '-': if (l instanceof Date) return r instanceof Date ? timespan(l - r) : new Date(l.getTime() - (r && r.__type === 'System.TimeSpan' ? r.TotalMilliseconds : toNum(r))); return toNum(l, true) - toNum(r, true); case '*': if (typeof l === 'string') return l.repeat(Math.max(0, toNum(r))); if (Array.isArray(l)) { let out = []; for (let i = 0; i < toNum(r); i++) out = out.concat(l); return out; } return toNum(l, true) * toNum(r, true); case '/': { const d = toNum(r, true); if (d === 0) throw new PSError('Attempted to divide by zero.', { id: 'RuntimeException', category: 'NotSpecified' }); return toNum(l, true) / d; } case '%': return toNum(l, true) % toNum(r, true); case 'eq': return scalarCmp((x) => looseEq(x, r, cs)); case 'ne': return scalarCmp((x) => !looseEq(x, r, cs)); case 'gt': return scalarCmp((x) => compare(x, r, cs) > 0); case 'ge': return scalarCmp((x) => compare(x, r, cs) >= 0); case 'lt': return scalarCmp((x) => compare(x, r, cs) < 0); case 'le': return scalarCmp((x) => compare(x, r, cs) <= 0); case 'like': { const re = TX.likeToRegex(toStr(r), cs); return scalarCmp((x) => re.test(toStr(x))); } case 'notlike': { const re = TX.likeToRegex(toStr(r), cs); return scalarCmp((x) => !re.test(toStr(x))); } case 'match': case 'notmatch': { const re = new RegExp(toStr(r), cs ? '' : 'i'); if (Array.isArray(l)) return l.filter((x) => re.test(toStr(x)) === (o === 'match')); const m = re.exec(toStr(l)); if (m) { const h = new PSHash(); m.forEach((g, i) => { if (g !== undefined) h.set(i, g); }); if (m.groups) Object.keys(m.groups).forEach((k) => h.set(k, m.groups[k])); rt.setVar('Matches', h); } return o === 'match' ? !!m : !m; } case 'contains': return unroll(l).some((x) => looseEq(x, r, cs)); case 'notcontains': return !unroll(l).some((x) => looseEq(x, r, cs)); case 'in': return unroll(r).some((x) => looseEq(x, l, cs)); case 'notin': return !unroll(r).some((x) => looseEq(x, l, cs)); case 'replace': { const [pat, rep] = Array.isArray(r) ? r : [r, '']; const re = new RegExp(toStr(pat), 'g' + (cs ? '' : 'i')); const f = (x) => toStr(x).replace(re, toStr(rep).replace(/\$(\d)/g, '$$$1')); return Array.isArray(l) ? l.map(f) : f(l); } case 'split': { const [pat, max] = Array.isArray(r) ? r : [r]; const re = new RegExp(toStr(pat), cs ? '' : 'i'); const res = []; unroll(l).forEach((x) => { let parts = toStr(x).split(re); if (max) { const n = toNum(max); if (parts.length > n) parts = parts.slice(0, n - 1).concat([parts.slice(n - 1).join(toStr(pat))]); } res.push(...parts); }); return res; } case 'join': return unroll(l).map((x) => toStr(x, rt)).join(toStr(r)); case 'f': return formatOp(toStr(l), r); case 'and': return truthy(l) && truthy(r); case 'or': return truthy(l) || truthy(r); case 'xor': return truthy(l) !== truthy(r); case 'band': return toNum(l) & toNum(r); case 'bor': return toNum(l) | toNum(r); case 'bxor': return toNum(l) ^ toNum(r); case 'shl': return toNum(l) << toNum(r); case 'shr': return toNum(l) >> toNum(r); case 'is': case 'isnot': { const t = r && r.__isType ? r.name : toStr(r); const ok = isType(l, t); return o === 'is' ? ok : !ok; } case 'as': { try { return castTo(r && r.__isType ? r.name : toStr(r), l, rt); } catch (e) { return null; } } } throw new PSError(`Unexpected token '-${op}' in expression or statement.`); } TX.psBinary = binaryOp; function isType(v, t) { t = t.toLowerCase().replace(/^system\./, ''); const tn = typeName(v).toLowerCase().replace(/^system\./, ''); const map = { int: ['int32', 'int64'], int32: ['int32'], long: ['int64', 'int32'], double: ['double'], string: ['string'], bool: ['boolean'], boolean: ['boolean'], array: ['object[]'], 'object[]': ['object[]'], datetime: ['datetime'], hashtable: ['collections.hashtable'], scriptblock: ['management.automation.scriptblock'], pscustomobject: ['management.automation.pscustomobject'], object: [tn], valuetype: ['int32', 'int64', 'double', 'boolean', 'datetime'] }; if (t === 'int' || t === 'int32') return typeof v === 'number' && Number.isInteger(v); if (t === 'double' || t === 'float' || t === 'decimal') return typeof v === 'number'; if (t === 'io.fileinfo' || t === 'fileinfo') return tn === 'io.fileinfo'; if (t === 'io.directoryinfo') return tn === 'io.directoryinfo'; return (map[t] || [t]).includes(tn); } function castTo(type, v, rt) { const t = type.toLowerCase().replace(/^system\./, '').trim(); const arr = /\[\]$/.test(t); if (arr) { const inner = t.slice(0, -2); return unroll(v).map((x) => castTo(inner, x, rt)); } switch (t) { case 'int': case 'int32': case 'int64': case 'long': case 'byte': case 'int16': case 'uint32': case 'uint64': { if (typeof v === 'string' && !/^\s*[-+]?[\d.]+(e\d+)?\s*$|^0x/i.test(v)) throw new PSError(`Cannot convert value "${v}" to type "System.Int32". Error: "The input string '${v}' was not in a correct format."`, { id: 'InvalidCastFromStringToInteger', category: 'InvalidArgument' }); const n = toNum(v); return Math.round(n) === n + 0.5 && Math.floor(n) % 2 === 0 ? Math.floor(n) : Math.round(n); } case 'double': case 'float': case 'single': case 'decimal': { const n = toNum(v); if (isNaN(n)) throw new PSError(`Cannot convert value "${toStr(v)}" to type "System.Double". Error: "The input string '${toStr(v)}' was not in a correct format."`); return n; } case 'string': return toStr(v, rt); case 'bool': case 'boolean': return truthy(v); case 'char': return typeof v === 'number' ? Error: "String '${toStr(v)}' was not recognized as a valid DateTime."`); return d; } case 'pscustomobject': case 'psobject': case 'management.automation.pscustomobject': { if (v instanceof PSHash) { const o = { __type: 'System.Management.Automation.PSCustomObject' }; v.entries().forEach(([k, val]) => { o[k] = val; }); return o; } return v; } case 'ordered': { if (v instanceof PSHash) { v.ordered = true; return v; } return v; } case 'hashtable': case 'collections.hashtable': return v instanceof PSHash ? v : new PSHash(); case 'regex': return new RegExp(toStr(v)); case 'scriptblock': return v; case 'math': case 'console': return v; case 'guid': return toStr(v); case 'version': return toStr(v); case 'xml': return v; case 'void': return undefined; case 'ipaddress': case 'net.ipaddress': return { __type: 'System.Net.IPAddress', IPAddressToString: toStr(v), AddressFamily: 'InterNetwork', __toString() { return toStr(v); } }; } return v; } TX.psCast = castTo; // ============================================================ formatting (Out-Default) const PS7_HDR = (s, rt) => (rt && rt.pwsh ? '\x1b[32;1m' + s + '\x1b[0m' : s); function cellStr(v, rt) { if (v === null || v === undefined) return ''; if (typeof v === 'number') return numStr(v); if (v instanceof Date) return TX.psDate(v.getTime()).replace(/ +/g, ' ').replace(/(\d+:\d+) ([AP]M)/, '$1:' + TX.p2(v.getSeconds()) + ' $2'); if (Array.isArray(v)) { const s = '{' + v.slice(0, 4).map((x) => cellStr(x, rt)).join(', ') + (v.length > 4 ? '…' : '') + '}'; return toStr(v, rt); } TX.psCellStr = cellStr; function renderTable(objs, cols, rt, opts = {}) { const W = (rt && rt.term ? rt.term.cols : 120) - 1; const rows = objs.map((o) => cols.map((c) => { const raw = c.get ? c.get(o, rt) : getMember(o, c.name, rt); return { raw, s: c.fmt ? c.fmt(raw, o, rt) : cellStr(raw, rt), color: c.color ? c.color(raw, o, rt) : null }; })); const widths = cols.map((c, i) => { if (c.width && !opts.auto) return c.width; let w = TX.visLen(c.label); rows.forEach((r) => { w = Math.max(w, TX.visLen(r[i].s)); }); return w; }); const right = cols.map((c, i) => c.align === 'right' || (c.align !== 'left' && rows.length && rows.every((r) => r[i].raw === null || r[i].raw === undefined || r[i].raw === '' || typeof r[i].raw === 'number'))); // fit to width: shrink the last column / truncate let total = widths.reduce((a, b) => a + b + 1, -1); if (total > W) { const lastI = widths.length - 1; const other = total - widths[lastI]; widths[lastI] = Math.max(8, W - other - 1); total = widths.reduce((a, b) => a + b + 1, -1); if (total > W) { let k = widths.length - 1; while (total > W && k >= 0) { const cut = Math.min(widths[k] - 4, total - W); if (cut > 0) { widths[k] -= cut; total -= cut; } k--; } } } const ell = rt && rt.pwsh ? '…' : '...'; const fit = (s, w, r) => { const len = TX.visLen(s); if (len > w) s = TX.stripAnsi(s).slice(0, Math.max(0, w - ell.length)) + ell; return r ? TX.padL(s, w) : TX.padR(s, w); }; const lines = []; const hdr = cols.map((c, i) => fit(c.label, widths[i], right[i])).join(' ').replace(/\s+$/, ''); const dash = cols.map((c, i) => fit('-'.repeat(Math.min(TX.visLen(c.label), widths[i])), widths[i], right[i])).join(' ').replace(/\s+$/, ''); if (cols.some((c) => c.label2)) { lines.push(PS7_HDR(cols.map((c, i) => fit(c.label, widths[i], right[i])).join(' ').replace(/\s+$/, ''), rt)); lines.push(PS7_HDR(cols.map((c, i) => fit(c.label2 || '', widths[i], right[i])).join(' ').replace(/\s+$/, ''), rt)); } else lines.push(PS7_HDR(hdr, rt)); lines.push(PS7_HDR(dash, rt)); rows.forEach((r) => { lines.push(r.map((cell, i) => { let s = fit(cell.s, widths[i], right[i]); if (cell.color && rt && rt.pwsh) { const t = s.replace(/\s+$/, ''); s = cell.color + t + '\x1b[0m' + s.slice(t.length); } return s; }).join(' ').replace(/\s+$/, '')); }); return lines.join('\n'); } function renderList(objs, props, rt) { const out = []; objs.forEach((o) => { const ps = props || listProps(o); const w = Math.max(...ps.map((p) => (typeof p === 'string' ? p : p.label).length)); const lines = ps.map((p) => { const label = typeof p === 'string' ? p : p.label; const v = typeof p === 'string' ? getMember(o, p, rt) : p.get(o, rt); let s = cellStr(v, rt); if (Array.isArray(v)) s = '{' + v.map((x) => cellStr(x, rt)).join(', ') + '}'; const indent = ' '.repeat(w + 3); s = String(s).split('\n').join('\n' + indent); return (rt && rt.pwsh ? '\n' : '') + o.text.replace(/^\n+/, '') + '\n\n'; } else s += o.text + '\n'; }); return s; } function formatGroup(group, rt, force) { const v = group[0]; if (v instanceof PSHash) { const rows = []; group.forEach((h) => h.entries().forEach(([k, val]) => rows.push({ Name: k, Value: val }))); return renderTable(rows, [{ label: 'Name', name: 'Name', width: Math.max(30, ...rows.map((r) => toStr(r.Name).length)) }, { label: 'Value', name: 'Value' }], rt, {}); } if (v instanceof PSScriptBlock) return group.map((x) => x.toString()).join('\n'); const view = VIEWS[typeName(v)]; if (view && !force) { if (view.custom) return view.custom(group, rt); if (view.table) { const cols = typeof view.table === 'function' ? let hit; if (cs.test.t === 'sb') { this.setVar('_', v); hit = truthy(collapse(await this.execBlock(cs.test.block.body))); } else { const tv = cs.test.t === 'str' ? cs.test.v : await this.evalExpr(cs.test); this.setVar('_', v); out.push(...(await this.execBlock(cs.body.body))); } } if (!matched) { const d = st.cases.find((c) => c.test.t === 'default'); if (d) { this.setVar('_', v); out.push(...(await this.execBlock(d.body.body))); } } } catch (e) { if (e instanceof Sig && e.kind === 'break') break; if (!(e instanceof Sig && e.kind === 'continue')) throw e; } } return out; } case 'function': this.functions.set(st.name.toLowerCase(), { name: st.name, params: st.params, block: st.block }); return []; case 'return': throw new Sig('return', st.v ? await this.execStatement(st.v) : []); case 'break': throw new Sig('break'); case 'continue': throw new Sig('continue'); case 'exit': throw new Sig('exit', st.v ? toNum(await this.evalExpr(st.v)) : 0); case 'throw': { const v = st.v ? collapse(await this.execStatement(st.v)) : 'ScriptHalted'; const e = new PSError(toStr(v), { id: toStr(v), category: 'OperationStopped', thrown: true, value: v }); throw e; } case 'try': { let out = []; try { out = await this.execBlock(st.body.body); } catch (e) { if (e instanceof Sig || e.interrupt || e.exitShell) throw e; const rec = this.errorRecord(e); const handler = st.catches.find((c) => !c.type || new RegExp(c.type.split('.').pop(), 'i').test(e.exType || 'RuntimeException') || /Exception$/.test(c.type) && c.type === 'System.Exception'); if (!handler) throw e; this.scopes.push(new Map()); this.setVar('_', rec, true); try { out = out.concat(await this.execBlock(handler.body.body)); } finally { this.scopes.pop(); } } finally { if (st.fin) out = out.concat(await this.execBlock(st.fin.body)); } return out; } case 'param': return []; } throw new PSError('Unsupported statement: ' + st.t); } errorRecord(e) { const msg = e.message || String(e); return { __type: 'System.Management.Automation.ErrorRecord', Exception: { __type: 'System.Management.Automation.RuntimeException', Message: msg, __toString() { return msg; } }, TargetObject: e.target || null, CategoryInfo: (e.category || 'NotSpecified') + ': (:) [], RuntimeException', FullyQualifiedErrorId: e.id || 'RuntimeException', InvocationInfo: null, ScriptStackTrace: 'at , : line 1', __toString() { return msg; } }; } async assignTo(target, v) { if (target.t === 'var') { this.setVar(target.name, v); return; } if (target.t === 'member') { const obj = await this.evalExpr(target.obj); const name = typeof target.name === 'string' ? target.name : toStr(await this.evalExpr(target.name)); setMember(obj, name, v); return; } if (target.t === 'index') { const obj = await this.evalExpr(target.obj); const idx = await this.evalExpr(target.idx); if (obj instanceof PSHash) { obj.set(idx, v); return; } if (Array.isArray(obj)) { let i = toNum(idx); if (i < 0) i += obj.length; if (i >= obj.length || i < 0) throw new PSError('Index was outside the bounds of the array.', { id: 'System.IndexOutOfRangeException', category: 'OperationStopped' }); obj[i] = v; return; } if (obj && typeof obj === 'object') { setMember(obj, toStr(idx), v); return; } throw new PSError('Unable to index into an object of type ' + typeName(obj) + '.'); } throw new PSError('The assignment expression is not valid. The input to an assignment operator must be an object that is able to accept assignments, such as a variable or a property.', { id: 'InvalidLeftHandSide' }); } async evalLValueGet(t) { return this.evalExpr(t); } async evalExpr(e) { switch (e.t) { case 'num': return e.v; case 'str': return e.v; case 'expstr': { let s = ''; for (const p of e.parts) { if (p.t === 'str') s += p.v; else if (p.t === 'var') s += toStr(this.getVar(p.name), this); else if (p.t === 'subexpr') s += toStr(collapse(await this.execBlock(p.body)), this); } return s; } case 'concat': { let s = ''; for (const p of e.parts) s += toStr(await this.evalExpr(p), this); return s; } case 'var': { if (!this.hasVar(e.name) && !/^(env:|_$|psitem$|pwd$|\?$|error$|home$|host$|args$|input$|matches$)/i.test(e.name) && this.strict) throw new PSError(`The variable '$${e.name}' cannot be retrieved because it has not been set.`); return this.getVar(e.name); } case 'array': { const out = []; for (const it of e.items) out.push(await this.evalExpr(it)); return out; } case 'arrsub': { const r = await this.execBlock(e.body); return r; } case 'subexpr': return collapse(await this.execBlock(e.body)); case 'paren': { const r = await this.execStatement(e.e); if (e.e.t === 'assign') { return collapse(unroll(await this.evalExpr(e.e.target))); } return e.e.t === 'pipeline' && e.e.elements.length === 1 && e.e.elements[0].t === 'expr' && e.e.elements[0].e.t === 'array' ? r : collapse(r); } case 'hash': { const h = new PSHash(); for (const en of e.entries) { const k = await this.evalExpr(en.key); const v = en.value.t === 'pipeline' && en.value.elements.length === 1 && en.value.elements[0].t === 'expr' ? await this.evalExpr(en.value.elements[0].e) : collapse(await this.execStatement(en.value)); h.set(toStr(k), v); } return h; } case 'sb': return new PSScriptBlock(e, this); case 'range': { const a = Math.trunc(toNum(await this.evalExpr(e.l), true)), b = Math.trunc(toNum(await this.evalExpr(e.r), true)); const out = []; if (Math.abs(b - a) > 1e6) throw new PSError('The range is too large.'); for (let i = a; a <= b ? i <= b : i >= b; a <= b ? i++ : i--) out.push(i); return out; } case 'not': return !truthy(await this.evalExpr(e.e)); case 'neg': { const v = await this.evalExpr(e.e); return e.op === '-' ? -toNum(v, true) : toNum(v, true); } case 'unop': { const v = await this.evalExpr(e.e); if (e.op === 'split') return unroll(v).flatMap((x) => toStr(x).trim().split(/\s+/)); if (e.op === 'join') return unroll(v).map((x) => toStr(x)).join(''); if (e.op === 'bnot') return ~toNum(v); break; } case 'bin': { if (e.op === 'and') { const l = truthy(await this.evalExpr(e.l)); return l ? truthy(await this.evalExpr(e.r)) : false; } if (e.op === 'or') { const l = truthy(await this.evalExpr(e.l)); return l ? true : truthy(await this.evalExpr(e.r)); } const l = await this.evalExpr(e.l); const r = e.r.t === 'type' ? { __isType: true, name: e.r.type } : await this.evalExpr(e.r); return binaryOp(e.op, l, r, this); } case 'incr': { const cur = toNum(await this.evalExpr(e.target)); const nv = e.op === '++' ? cur + 1 : cur - 1; await this.assignTo(e.target, nv); return e.prefix ? nv : cur; } case 'cast': return castTo(e.type, await this.evalExpr(e.e), this); case 'type': return { __isType: true, name: e.type, __type: 'System.RuntimeType', __toString() { return TX.psResolveTypeName(e.type); } }; case 'static': return TX.psStatic(e.type, e.member, null, this); case 'member': { const obj = await this.evalExpr(e.obj); const name = typeof e.name === 'string' ? e.name : toStr(await this.evalExpr(e.name)); if (e.static || (obj && obj.__isType)) return TX.psStatic(obj.name || e.obj.type, name, null, this); if (obj === null && e.nullSafe) return null; const r = getMember(obj, name, this); return r === undefined ? null : r; } case 'call': { const obj = e.obj.t === 'type' ? { __isType: true, name: e.obj.type } : await this.evalExpr(e.obj); const args = []; for (const a of e.args) args.push(await this.evalExpr(a)); const name = typeof e.name === 'string' ? e.name : toStr(await this.evalExpr(e.name)); if (e.static || (obj && obj.__isType)) return TX.psStatic(obj.name, name, args, this); if (obj === null && e.nullSafe) return null; const lname = name.toLowerCase(); if (Array.isArray(obj) && (lname === 'where' || lname === 'foreach') && args[0] instanceof PSScriptBlock) { const res = []; for (const x of obj) { const r = collapse(await this.invokeBlockAsync(args[0], [], x)); if (lname === 'where') { if (truthy(r)) res.push(x); } else res.push(...unroll(r)); } return res; } if (obj instanceof PSScriptBlock && (lname === 'invoke' || lname === 'invokereturnasis')) return collapse(await this.invokeBlockAsync(obj, args, null)); return callMethod(obj, name, args, this); } case 'index': { const obj = await this.evalExpr(e.obj); const idx = await this.evalExpr(e.idx); const one = (i) => { if (obj === null || obj === undefined) throw new PSError('Cannot index into a null array.', { id: 'NullArray', category: 'InvalidOperation' }); if (obj instanceof PSHash) return obj.get(i) ?? null; if (typeof obj === 'string') { let k = toNum(i); if (k < 0) k += obj.length; return obj[k] ?? null; } if (Array.isArray(obj)) { let k = toNum(i); if (k < 0) k += obj.length; return obj[k] ?? null; } if (typeof obj === 'object') { const r = getMember(obj, toStr(i), this); if (r !== null && r !== undefined) return r; if (toNum(i) === 0) return obj; return null; } if (toNum(i) === 0) return obj; return null; }; if (Array.isArray(idx)) return idx.map(one); return one(idx); } case 'splat': return this.getVar(e.name); case 'paramdef': return null; case 'incrstmt': return null; } if (e.t === 'pipeline' || e.t === 'assign' || e.t === 'if' || e.t === 'chain') return collapse(await this.execStatement(e)); throw new PSError('Unsupported expression: ' + e.t); } // ---------- pipelines & commands async runPipeline(pl, input, opts = {}) { const n = pl.elements.length; let data = input; const redirs = pl.redirs || []; const toFile = redirs.find((r) => /^>>?$|^1>>?$/.test(r.op)); const errFile = redirs.find((r) => /^2>>?$/.test(r.op)); const allFile = redirs.find((r) => /^\*>>?$/.test(r.op)); const mergeErr = redirs.some((r) => r.op === '2>&1'); const savedSink = this.errSink; let errBuf = null; if (errFile || allFile) { errBuf = []; this.errSink = (rec) => errBuf.push(rec); } else if (mergeErr) this.errSink = (rec) => { this.mergedErrors = this.mergedErrors || []; this.mergedErrors.push(rec); }; let result; try { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const el = pl.elements[i]; const last = i === n - 1; if (el.t === 'expr') { if (i > 0) throw new PSError('Expressions are only allowed as the first element of a pipeline.', { id: 'ExpressionsMustBeFirstInPipeline', category: 'ParserError' }); const v = await this.evalExpr(el.e); data = el.e.t === 'arrsub' || el.e.t === 'array' ? v : unroll(v); if (!(v === null || v === undefined) && !Array.isArray(v)) data = [v]; if (el.e.t === 'paren' && Array.isArray(v)) data = v; this.lastOk = true; } else { data = await this.invoke(el, i === 0 ? null : data, { last, streamToHost: last && opts.toHost && !toFile && !allFile, first: i === 0 }); } } if (this.mergedErrors) { data = (data || []).concat(this.mergedErrors.map((r) => r.text)); this.mergedErrors = null; } result = data || []; } finally { this.errSink = savedSink; } const writeTarget = async (r, content) => { const target = toStr(await this.evalExpr(r.target), this); if (/^\$null$|^null$/i.test(target)) return; const segs = this.shell.resolve(target); try { this.shell.vfs.writeFile(segs, content, { append: r.op.endsWith('>>'), user: this.shell.fsUser(), owner: 'student' }); } catch (e) { this.writeError(e.code === 'ENOENT' ? `Could not find a part of the path '${TX.Paths.win.format(segs)}'.` : `Access to the path '${TX.Paths.win.format(segs)}' is denied.`, { cmd: 'out-file' }); } }; if (errFile) await writeTarget(errFile, errBuf.map((r) => r.plain + '\n').join('')); if (toFile || allFile) { const text = TX.stripAnsi(formatForHost(result, Object.assign(Object.create(this), { pwsh: false }))).replace(/^\n/, ''); await writeTarget(toFile || allFile, text + (allFile && errBuf ? errBuf.map((r) => r.plain + '\n').join('') : '')); return []; } return result; } resolveCommand(name) { let n = name.toLowerCase(); const seen = new Set(); while (this.aliases.has(n) && !seen.has(n)) { seen.add(n); n = this.aliases.get(n).toLowerCase(); } if (!seen.size && TX.psAliases[n] && !(this.pwsh && TX.psAliasesRemoved7.includes(n))) n = TX.psAliases[n].toLowerCase(); else if (seen.size && TX.psAliases[n]) n = TX.psAliases[n].toLowerCase(); if (this.functions.has(n)) return { kind: 'function', def: this.functions.get(n), name: this.functions.get(n).name }; if (TX.psCmdlets[n]) return { kind: 'cmdlet', def: TX.psCmdlets[n], name: TX.psCmdlets[n].name }; const exe = n.replace(/\.exe$/, ''); if (TX.winExe[exe] || ['git', 'curl', 'tar', 'ssh', 'cmd', 'powershell', 'pwsh', 'wsl', 'bash', 'python', 'python3', 'py', 'node', 'where'].includes(exe)) return { kind: 'native', name: exe }; return null; } async evalArgs(args) { const out = []; for (const a of args) { if (a.param !== undefined) { if (a.value !== undefined) out.push({ param: a.param, value: await this.argValue(a.value), colon: true }); else out.push({ param: a.param }); } else if (a.value.t === 'splat') { const h = this.getVar(a.value.name); if (h instanceof PSHash) h.entries().forEach(([k, v]) => out.push({ param: k, value: v, colon: true })); else unroll(h).forEach((v) => out.push({ value: v })); } else out.push({ value: await this.argValue(a.value), raw: a.value }); } return out; } async argValue(node) { if (node.t === 'str') return node.v; if (node.t === 'array') { const r = []; for (const it of node.items) r.push(await this.argValue(it)); return r; } return this.evalExpr(node); } async invoke(el, input, flags) { let nameVal = el.name.t === 'str' ? el.name.v : await this.evalExpr(el.name); const args = await this.evalArgs(el.args); if (nameVal instanceof PSScriptBlock) return this.invokeScriptBlockCmd(nameVal, args, input, el.callOp === '.'); const name = toStr(nameVal); const res = this.resolveCommand(name); this.currentCmd = el; if (!res) { // script / exe path? if (/[\\/]/.test(name) || /\.(ps1|bat|cmd|exe|txt|py)$/i.test(name)) return this.invokePath(name, args, input, flags, el); const segs = this.shell.resolve(name); const node = this.shell.vfs.get(segs); if (node && node.t === 'f' && /\.ps1$/i.test(name)) { this.writeError(`The term '${name}' is not recognized as a name of a cmdlet, function, script file, or executable program.\nCheck the spelling of the name, or if a path was included, verify that the path is correct and try again.\n\nSuggestion [3,General]: The command "${name}" was not found, but does exist in the current location. PowerShell does not load commands from the current location by default. If you trust this command, instead type: ".\\${name}". See "get-help about_Command_Precedence" for more details.`, { cmd: name, notFound: true }); return []; } const s = TX.suggest(name, Object.values(TX.psCmdlets).map((c) => c.name).concat(Object.keys(TX.psAliases)), 2); this.writeError(`The term '${name}' is not recognized as ${this.pwsh ? 'a name of a' : 'the name of a'} cmdlet, function, script file, or ${this.pwsh ? 'executable' : 'operable'} program.${this.pwsh ? '\n' : ' '}Check the spelling of the name, or if a path was included, verify that the path is correct and try again.${s && this.pwsh ? `\n\n\x1b[0mSuggestion [4,General]: Did you mean: ${s}?` : ''}`, { cmd: name, notFound: true, category: 'ObjectNotFound', id: 'CommandNotFoundException' }); this.lastOk = false; return []; } if (res.kind === 'function') return this.invokeFunction(res.def, args, input); if (res.kind === 'native') return this.invokeNative(res.name, args, input, flags); return this.invokeCmdlet(res.def, args, input, flags, el); } bind(def, args) { const spec = def.params || {}; const names = Object.keys(spec); const p = { _positional: [] }; const common = { erroraction: 'ErrorAction', ea: 'ErrorAction', warningaction: 'WarningAction', wa: 'WarningAction', verbose: 'Verbose', vb: 'Verbose', debug: 'Debug', whatif: 'WhatIf', wi: 'WhatIf', confirm: 'Confirm', cf: 'Confirm', outvariable: 'OutVariable', ov: 'OutVariable', errorvariable: 'ErrorVariable', ev: 'ErrorVariable', informationaction: 'InformationAction', outbuffer: 'OutBuffer', pipelinevariable: 'PipelineVariable' }; const findParam = (nm) => { const ln = nm.toLowerCase(); const exact = names.find((k) => k.toLowerCase() === ln || (spec[k].alias || []).some((a) => a.toLowerCase() === ln)); if (exact) return exact; if (common[ln]) return '@' + common[ln]; const pref = names.filter((k) => k.toLowerCase().startsWith(ln)); if (pref.length === 1) return pref[0]; if (pref.length > 1) throw new PSError(`Parameter cannot be processed because the parameter name '${nm}' is ambiguous. Possible matches include: ${pref.map((x) => '-' + x).join(' ')}.`, { id: 'AmbiguousParameter', category: 'InvalidArgument' }); const cm = Object.keys(common).filter((k) => k.startsWith(ln)); if (cm.length) return '@' + common[cm[0]]; return null; }; for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) { const a = args[i]; if (a.param !== undefined) { if (def.rawParams) { p._positional.push('-' + a.param); if (a.colon) p._positional.push(a.value); continue; } const k = findParam(a.param); if (!k) { if (def.freeParams) { p._free = p._free || []; p._free.push({ param: a.param, value: a.colon ? a.value : undefined }); if (!a.colon && args[i + 1] && args[i + 1].param === undefined && def.freeParams === 'value') { p._free[p._free.length - 1].value = args[++i].value; } continue; } throw new PSError(`A parameter cannot be found that matches parameter name '${a.param}'.`, { id: 'NamedParameterNotFound', category: 'InvalidArgument' }); } if (k.startsWith('@')) { const cn = k.slice(1); if (a.colon) p[cn] = a.value; else if (['Verbose', 'Debug', 'WhatIf', 'Confirm'].includes(cn)) p[cn] = true; else { p[cn] = args[i + 1] ? args[++i].value : null; } continue; } if (spec[k].sw) { p[k] = a.colon ? truthy(a.value) : true; continue; } if (a.colon) { p[k] = a.value; continue; } const nx = args[i + 1]; if (!nx || nx.param !== undefined) throw new PSError(`Missing an argument for parameter '${k}'. Specify a parameter of type '${spec[k].type || 'System.Object'}' and try again.`, { id: 'MissingArgument', category: 'InvalidArgument' }); p[k] = nx.value; i++; continue; } p._positional.push(a.value); } // positional binding const posParams = names.filter((k) => spec[k].pos !== undefined && p[k] === undefined).sort((a, b) => spec[a].pos - spec[b].pos); const rest = []; p._positional.forEach((v) => { const k = posParams.shift(); if (k) p[k] = v; else rest.push(v); }); if (rest.length && !def.rest && !def.rawParams) throw new PSError(`A positional parameter cannot be found that accepts argument '${toStr(rest[0])}'.`, { id: 'PositionalParameterNotFound', category: 'InvalidArgument' }); p._rest = rest; return p; } async invokeCmdlet(def, args, input, flags, el) { let p; try { p = this.bind(def, args); } catch (e) { this.writeError(e.message, { cmd: def.name, category: e.category, id: e.id }); this.lastOk = false; return []; } const ea = p.ErrorAction ? toStr(p.ErrorAction).toLowerCase() : toStr(this.getVar('ErrorActionPreference')).toLowerCase(); const savedEA = this.ea; this.ea = ea; let hadError = false; const ctx = { rt: this, cmd: def.name, flags, el, error: (msg, o = {}) => { hadError = true; if (ea === 'stop') throw new PSError(msg, Object.assign({ cmd: def.name }, o)); if (ea === 'silentlycontinue' || ea === 'ignore') { this.errors.push(this.errorRecord(new PSError(msg, o))); return; } this.writeError(msg, Object.assign({ cmd: def.name }, o)); }, warn: (msg) => { if (p.WarningAction && /silently/i.test(toStr(p.WarningAction))) return; this.host(C.style(['yellow'], (this.pwsh ? 'WARNING: ' : 'WARNING: ') + msg) + '\n'); }, whatIf: (op, target) => { if (p.WhatIf) { this.host(`What if: Performing the operation "${op}" on target "${target}".\n`); return true; } return false; }, }; try { const out = await def.fn(p, input, ctx); this.lastOk = !hadError; if (p.OutVariable) this.setVar(toStr(p.OutVariable), collapse(out || [])); return out || []; } catch (e) { if (e instanceof Sig || e.interrupt || e.exitShell) throw e; if (e instanceof TX.FsError) { ctx.error(TX.psFsMsg(e)); this.lastOk = false; return []; } if (e.ps && !e.thrown && ea !== 'stop') { this.writeError(e.message, { cmd: def.name, category: e.category, id: e.id }); this.lastOk = false; return []; } throw e; } finally { this.ea = savedEA; } } async invokeFunction(fn, args, input) { const scope = new Map(); this.scopes.push(scope); try { let body = fn.block.body; let params = fn.params; if (!params) { const ps = body.find((s) => s.t === 'param'); if (ps) params = ps.params; } const named = {}, positional = []; for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) { const a = args[i]; if (a.param !== undefined) { const k = (params || []).find((pp) => pp.name.toLowerCase() === a.param.toLowerCase() || pp.name.toLowerCase().startsWith(a.param.toLowerCase())); if (!k) { if (!params) { positional.push('-' + a.param); continue; } throw new PSError(`A parameter cannot be found that matches parameter name '${a.param}'.`, { id: 'NamedParameterNotFound', category: 'InvalidArgument' }); } if (/switch/i.test(k.type || '')) named[k.name] = a.colon ? truthy(a.value) : true; else named[k.name] = a.colon ? a.value : (args[i + 1] ? args[++i].value : null); } else positional.push(a.value); } const argsLeft = []; for (const pp of params || []) { let v; if (named[pp.name] !== undefined) v = named[pp.name]; else if (positional.length && !/switch/i.test(pp.type || '')) v = positional.shift(); else if (pp.def) v = await this.evalExpr(pp.def); else v = /switch/i.test(pp.type || '') ? false : null; if (pp.type && v !== null) { try { v = castTo(pp.type, v, this); } catch (e) { throw new PSError(`Cannot process argument transformation on parameter '${pp.name}'. ${e.message}`, { id: 'ParameterArgumentTransformationError', category: 'InvalidData' }); } } this.setVar(pp.name, v, true); } argsLeft.push(...positional); this.setVar('args', argsLeft, true); this.setVar('input', input || [], true); if (input && input.length && fn.isFilter !== false) this.setVar('_', input[input.length - 1], true); try { return await this.execBlock(body); } catch (e) { if (e instanceof Sig && e.kind === 'return') return e.value.concat ? e.value : [e.value]; throw e; } } finally { this.scopes.pop(); } } async invokeScriptBlockCmd(sb, args, input, dot) { if (dot) return this.execBlock(sb.node.body); return this.invokeFunction({ block: { body: sb.node.body }, params: sb.node.params }, args, input); } // used by Where-Object/ForEach-Object & friends: run sb with $_ bound async invokeBlockAsync(sb, args, item) { // script blocks run by Where-Object/ForEach-Object/Sort-Object execute in the caller's scope ($_ is temporary) const top = this.scopes[this.scopes.length - 1]; const saved = ['_', 'psitem', 'args'].map((k) => [k, top.get(k)]); top.set('_', { name: '_', value: item }); top.set('psitem', { name: 'PSItem', value: item }); top.set('args', { name: 'args', value: args || [] }); let pushed = false; if (sb.node.params && sb.node.params.length) { this.scopes.push(new Map()); pushed = true; sb.node.params.forEach((pp, i) => this.setVar(pp.name, args && args[i] !== undefined ? args[i] : null, true)); } try { return await this.execBlock(sb.node.body); } catch (e) { if (e instanceof Sig && e.kind === 'return') return e.value; throw e; } finally { if (pushed) this.scopes.pop(); saved.forEach(([k, v]) => { if (v) top.set(k, v); else top.delete(k); }); } } invokeBlock() { throw new PSError('Synchronous script block invocation (.Where/.ForEach methods) is not supported in TerminalEX — use Where-Object / ForEach-Object instead.'); } async invokeNative(name, args, input, flags) { const strArgs = []; args.forEach((a) => { if (a.param !== undefined) { strArgs.push('-' + a.param + (a.colon ? ':' + toStr(a.value) : '')); } else unroll(a.value).forEach((v) => strArgs.push(toStr(v, this))); }); const stdinText = input ? input.map((x) => toStr(x, this)).join('\n') + '\n' : null; let buf = ''; const stream = flags.streamToHost; const io = { tty: stream, stdin: stdinText, out: stream ? C.brightYellow(m[6]) : m[6]); cmdPos = false; } else if (m[7]) { out.push(m[7] === '|' || m[7] === ';' || m[7] === '{' || m[7] === '(' ? C.brightYellow(w) : C.red(w)); cmdPos = false; } else out.push(w); } else out.push(m[0]); } return out.join(''); } completer(buf, pos) { const left = buf.slice(0, pos); const m = /(\S*)$/.exec(left); const word = m[1]; const start = pos - word.length; const before = left.slice(0, start).trim(); const cmdPos = !before || /[|;({]$/.test(before); // variables if (word.startsWith('$')) { const w = word.slice(1).toLowerCase(); if (w.startsWith('env:')) { const k = w.slice(4); return { start, mode: 'cycle', cands: Object.keys(this.env).filter((x) => x.toLowerCase().startsWith(k)).sort().map((x) => '$env:' + x) }; } const names = this.rt.allVars().map((v) => v.name).concat(['env:']).filter((n) => n.toLowerCase().startsWith(w)).sort(); return { start, mode: 'cycle', cands: names.map((n) => '$' + n) }; } // parameters if (word.startsWith('-') && !cmdPos) { const cmdName = (/(?:^|[|;{(]\s*)([\w-]+)[^|;{(]*$/.exec(before) || [])[1]; const res = cmdName ? this.rt.resolveCommand(cmdName) : null; if (res && res.kind === 'cmdlet') { const ps = Object.keys(res.def.params || {}).concat(['ErrorAction', 'WarningAction', 'Verbose', 'WhatIf', 'OutVariable']); return { start, mode: 'cycle', cands: ps.filter((x) => x.toLowerCase().startsWith(word.slice(1).toLowerCase())).map((x) => '-' + x) }; } } if (cmdPos && !/[\\/.]/.test(word)) { const lw = word.toLowerCase(); const cands = new Set(); Object.values(CMDLETS).forEach((c) => { if (c.name.toLowerCase().startsWith(lw)) cands.add(c.name); }); [...this.rt.functions.values()].forEach((f) => { if (f.name.toLowerCase().startsWith(lw)) cands.add(f.name); }); if (word.length >= 1) Object.keys(ALIASES).forEach((a) => { if (a.startsWith(lw) && a.length > 1) cands.add(a); }); Object.keys(TX.winExe).forEach((x) => { if (x.startsWith(lw)) cands.add(x + '.exe'); }); const list = [...cands].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'en', { sensitivity: 'base' })); if (list.length) return { start, mode: 'cycle', cands: list }; } // paths const quoted = word.startsWith("'") || word.startsWith('"'); const w = word.replace(/^['"]/, ''); const sep = Math.max(w.lastIndexOf('\\'), w.lastIndexOf('/')); const dirPart = sep >= 0 ? w.slice(0, sep + 1) : ''; const base = sep >= 0 ? w.slice(sep + 1) : w; const dirSegs = dirPart ? this.resolve(dirPart) : this.cwd; const node = this.vfs.get(dirSegs); if (!node || node.t !== 'd') return null; const dirsOnly = /^(cd|set-location|sl|chdir|pushd|push-location)$/i.test(before); const cands = Object.keys(node.ch).filter((n) => n.toLowerCase().startsWith(base.toLowerCase()) && (!dirsOnly || node.ch[n].t === 'd') && (!node.ch[n].hid || base.startsWith('.'))).sort((a, b) => (node.ch[a].t === 'd' ? 0 : 1) - (node.ch[b].t === 'd' ? 0 : 1) || a.localeCompare(b, 'en', { sensitivity: 'base' })) .map((n) => { let full = (dirPart || (quoted ? '' : '.\\')) + n + (node.ch[n].t === 'd' ? '\\' : ''); if (/\s/.test(full) || quoted) full = "'" + full + "'"; return full; }); return { start, mode: 'cycle', cands }; } } TX.PowerShell = PowerShell; })(); // ---- storage.js ---- /* TerminalEX — disks and RAID. * Every machine that has RAID tools gets two blank "lab" disks to practice on, so nothing a student does can touch the * system disk: * Linux mdadm (RAID 0/1/5), /proc/mdstat, mkfs.ext4, mount, and failing/replacing a disk * Windows diskpart (online/offline, attributes, mirror/stripe volumes) and PowerShell Storage Spaces * (Get-Disk, Set-Disk, Initialize-Disk, Get-PhysicalDisk, New-StoragePool, New-VirtualDisk ...) * Solaris / FreeBSD zpool create mirror|raidz, offline/online a disk; FreeBSD gmirror */ (function () { 'use strict'; const TX = window.TX; const C = TX.C; const G = TX.gb; const cmds = TX.posixCmds; const def = TX.defPosix; const isRoot = (c) => c.user === 'root'; const human = (b) => TX.human(b); // ------------------------------------------------------------------ the lab disks TX.DISKS.linux.push( { dev: 'sdc', model: 'WDC WD10EZEX (lab disk 1)', size: G(931.5), type: 'disk', tran: 'sata', rota: 1, serial: 'WD-LAB0001', lab: true, parts: [] }, { dev: 'sdd', model: 'WDC WD10EZEX (lab disk 2)', size: G(931.5), type: 'disk', tran: 'sata', rota: 1, serial: 'WD-LAB0002', lab: true, parts: [] }); TX.DISKS.windows.push( { num: 3, model: 'WDC WD10EZEX-00BBHA0 (lab disk 1)', size: G(931.51), bus: 'SATA', media: 'HDD', health: 'Healthy', style: 'RAW', serial: 'WD-WCC6Y4KX0001', lab: true, offline: true, parts: [] }, { num: 4, model: 'WDC WD10EZEX-00BBHA0 (lab disk 2)', size: G(931.51), bus: 'SATA', media: 'HDD', health: 'Healthy', style: 'RAW', serial: 'WD-WCC6Y4KX0002', lab: true, offline: true, parts: [] }); TX.DISKS.solaris.push({ dev: 'c2t0d0', model: 'HGST HUS726T4TALA6L4 (lab)', size: G(3726), lab: true }, { dev: 'c2t1d0', model: 'HGST HUS726T4TALA6L4 (lab)', size: G(3726), lab: true }); TX.DISKS.freebsd.push({ dev: 'ada1', model: 'WDC WD10EZEX (lab disk 1)', size: G(931.5), lab: true }, { dev: 'ada2', model: 'WDC WD10EZEX (lab disk 2)', size: G(931.5), lab: true }); // ================================================================== Linux: mdadm const arrays = (m) => (m.extra.md = m.extra.md || {}); const devName = (s) => String(s || '').replace(/^\/dev\//, ''); const RESYNC_MS = 45000; function mdProgress(a) { return a.syncStart ? Math.min(1, (Date.now() - a.syncStart) / RESYNC_MS) : 1; } function mdState(a) { const bad = a.devices.filter((d) => d.state === 'faulty').length; if (a.level === 0 && bad) return 'FAILED'; const need = a.level === 5 ? a.n - 1 : a.level === 1 ? 1 : a.n; if (a.devices.filter((d) => d.state === 'active').length < need) return 'FAILED'; const degraded = a.devices.filter((d) => d.state === 'active').length < a.n; const p = mdProgress(a); return (degraded ? 'clean, degraded' : 'clean') + (p < 1 ? (a.recovering ? ', recovering' : ', resyncing') : ''); } const blocks = (a) => Math.round((a.level === 0 ? a.size * a.n : a.level === 5 ? a.size * (a.n - 1) : a.size) / 1024); const prevGens = TX.MACHINE_KINDS.linux.gens; TX.MACHINE_KINDS.linux.gens = (m) => { const g = prevGens ? prevGens(m) : TX.machineGens('linux', m); g.mdstat = () => { const A = Object.values(arrays(m)); let s = 'Personalities : ' + [...new Set(A.map((a) => '[raid' + a.level + ']'))].join(' ') + '\n'; A.forEach((a) => { const st = mdState(a); s += `${a.name} : ${st === 'FAILED' ? 'inactive' : 'active'} raid${a.level} ${a.devices.map((d, i) => `${d.dev}[${i}]${d.state === 'faulty' ? '(F)' : d.state === 'spare' ? '(S)' : ''}`).reverse().join(' ')}\n`; s += ` ${blocks(a)} blocks super 1.2 ${a.level === 0 ? '512k chunks' : `[${a.n}/${a.devices.filter((d) => d.state === 'active').length}] [${a.devices.slice(0, a.n).map((d) => (d.state === 'active' ? 'U' : '_')).join('')}]`}\n`; const p = mdProgress(a); if (p < 1 && a.level !== 0) { const w = 20, k = Math.floor(p * w); s += ` [${'='.repeat(k)}>${'.'.repeat(w - k)}] ${a.recovering ? 'recovery' : 'resync'} = ${(p * 100).toFixed(1)}% (${Math.round(blocks(a) * p)}/${blocks(a)}) finish=${((1 - p) * RESYNC_MS / 60000).toFixed(1)}min speed=187520K/sec\n`; } s += '\n'; }); return s + 'unused devices: \n'; }; return g; }; def('mdadm', async (c) => { const a = c.args; const A = arrays(c.m); const opt = (long, short) => { const i = a.findIndex((x) => x === long || x === short || x.startsWith(long + '=') || (short && x.startsWith(short) && x.length > 2 && !x.startsWith('--'))); if (i < 0) return undefined; const x = a[i]; if (x.includes('=')) return x.split('=')[1]; if (short && x.startsWith(short) && x !== short) return x.slice(short.length); return a[i + 1]; }; const has = (...f) => a.some((x) => f.includes(x)); if (!a.length || has('--help', '-h')) { c.outln('Usage: mdadm --create /dev/md0 --level=1 --raid-devices=2 /dev/sdc /dev/sdd\n mdadm --detail /dev/md0\n mdadm /dev/md0 --fail /dev/sdc (pretend a disk broke)\n mdadm /dev/md0 --remove /dev/sdc\n mdadm /dev/md0 --add /dev/sdc (put a new disk in: it rebuilds)\n mdadm --stop /dev/md0\n cat /proc/mdstat (watch the arrays)\n' + C.dim('Practice disks: /dev/sdc and /dev/sdd (both blank). The system disk is protected.')); return 0; } const needRoot = () => { if (isRoot(c)) return false; c.err('mdadm: must be super-user to perform this action\n' + C.dim('(try sudo mdadm ... )') + '\n'); return true; }; if (has('--detail', '-D')) { const name = devName(a.find((x) => /md\d+/.test(x))); const r = A[name]; if (!r) { c.err(`mdadm: cannot open /dev/${name || 'md0'}: No such file or directory\n`); return 1; } const act = r.devices.filter((d) => d.state === 'active').length; c.outln(`/dev/${r.name}:\n Version : 1.2\n Creation Time : ${TX.unixDate(r.created)}\n Raid Level : raid${r.level}\n Array Size : ${blocks(r)} (${human(blocks(r) * 1024)} ${(blocks(r) * 1024 / 1e9).toFixed(2)} GB)\n${r.level ? ` Used Dev Size : ${Math.round(r.size / 1024)} (${human(r.size)})\n` : ''} Raid Devices : ${r.n}\n Total Devices : ${r.devices.length}\n\n State : ${mdState(r)}\n Active Devices : ${act}\n Working Devices : ${r.devices.filter((d) => d.state !== 'faulty').length}\n Failed Devices : ${r.devices.filter((d) => d.state === 'faulty').length}\n Spare Devices : ${r.devices.filter((d) => d.state === 'spare').length}\n${mdProgress(r) < 1 ? `\n ${r.recovering ? 'Rebuild' : 'Resync'} Status : ${Math.floor(mdProgress(r) * 100)}% complete\n` : ''}\n Number Major Minor RaidDevice State`); r.devices.forEach((d, i) => c.outln(` ${i} 8 ${String(32 + i * 16).padEnd(7)} ${String(d.state === 'faulty' ? '-' : i).padStart(2)} ${d.state === 'active' ? 'active sync' : d.state === 'faulty' ? 'faulty' : 'spare rebuilding'} /dev/${d.dev}`)); return 0; } if (has('--create', '-C')) { if (needRoot()) return 1; const name = devName(opt('--create', '-C')); const level = parseInt(String(opt('--level', '-l') || '').replace(/^raid/, ''), 10); const n = parseInt(opt('--raid-devices', '-n'), 10); const devs = a.filter((x) => /^\/dev\/sd[a-z]+$/.test(x)).map(devName); if (!/^md\d+$/.test(name)) { c.err('mdadm: an md device must be given in this mode (like /dev/md0)\n'); return 2; } if (A[name]) { c.err(`mdadm: /dev/${name} is already in use.\n`); return 1; } if (![0, 1, 5].includes(level)) { c.err('mdadm: a RAID level is needed to create an array: --level=0 (stripe), 1 (mirror) or 5 (parity).\n'); return 2; } if (!n || n !== devs.length) { c.err(`mdadm: You have listed ${devs.length} devices but --raid-devices=${n || '?'}. They must match.\n`); return 2; } if (level === 5 && n < 3) { c.err('mdadm: at least 3 raid-devices needed for level 5\n' + C.dim('(RAID 5 stores parity across 3+ disks. This lab has two spare disks, so try RAID 1 or RAID 0.)') + '\n'); return 2; } if (n < 2) { c.err(`mdadm: at least 2 raid-devices needed for level ${level}\n`); return 2; } for (const d of devs) { const disk = c.m.disks.find((x) => x.dev === d); if (!disk) { c.err(`mdadm: cannot open /dev/${d}: No such file or directory\n`); return 1; } if (!disk.lab) { c.err(`mdadm: cannot open /dev/${d}: Device or resource busy\n` + C.dim(`(/dev/${d} holds this computer's files! Use the blank lab disks /dev/sdc and /dev/sdd.)`) + '\n'); return 1; } if (Object.values(A).some((r) => r.devices.some((x) => x.dev === d))) { c.err(`mdadm: /dev/${d} is already part of an array.\n`); return 1; } } if (level === 1) { c.outln('mdadm: Note: this array has metadata at the start and\n may not be suitable as a boot device. If you plan to\n store \'/boot\' on this device please ensure that\n your boot-loader understands md/v1.x metadata, or use\n --metadata=0.90'); c.outln(`mdadm: size set to ${Math.round(c.m.disks.find((x) => x.dev === devs[0]).size / 1024)}K`); if (c.tty && !has('--run', '-R')) { let ans = 'y'; try { ans = await c.readLine('Continue creating array? '); } catch (e) { ans = 'n'; } if (!/^y/i.test(ans)) { c.err('mdadm: create aborted.\n'); return 1; } } } const size = Math.min(...devs.map((d) => c.m.disks.find((x) => x.dev === d).size)); A[name] = { name, level, n, size, devices: devs.map((d) => ({ dev: d, state: 'active' })), created: Date.now(), syncStart: level === 0 ? 0 : Date.now(), fs: '', mount: '' }; c.outln('mdadm: Defaulting to version 1.2 metadata\nmdadm: array /dev/' + name + ' started.'); c.err(C.dim(level === 1 ? '(RAID 1 = mirror: every write goes to BOTH disks. Watch it copy with cat /proc/mdstat )' : '(RAID 0 = stripe: twice the space and speed, but if ONE disk dies, EVERYTHING is lost.)') + '\n'); c.vfs.changed(); return 0; } if (has('--stop', '-S')) { if (needRoot()) return 1; const name = devName(a.find((x) => /md\d+/.test(x))); if (!A[name]) { c.err(`mdadm: error opening /dev/${name}: No such file or directory\n`); return 1; } if (A[name].mount) { c.err(`mdadm: Cannot get exclusive access to /dev/${name}:Perhaps a running process, mounted filesystem or active volume group?\n` + C.dim('(unmount it first: sudo umount ' + A[name].mount + ')') + '\n'); return 1; } delete A[name]; c.outln(`mdadm: stopped /dev/${name}`); c.vfs.changed(); return 0; } // manage mode: mdadm /dev/md0 --fail /dev/sdc (also --manage) const name = devName(a.find((x) => /md\d+/.test(x))); const r = A[name]; const dev = devName(a.find((x) => /^\/dev\/sd[a-z]+$/.test(x))); if (has('--fail', '-f', '--remove', '-r', '--add', '-a')) { if (needRoot()) return 1; if (!r) { c.err(`mdadm: cannot open /dev/${name || 'md0'}: No such file or directory\n`); return 1; } const d = r.devices.find((x) => x.dev === dev); if (has('--fail', '-f')) { if (!d) { c.err(`mdadm: ${dev}: not a member of /dev/${name}\n`); return 1; } d.state = 'faulty'; c.outln(`mdadm: set /dev/${dev} faulty in /dev/${name}`); c.err(C.dim(r.level === 0 ? '(RAID 0 has no copy: the whole array is now FAILED and its files are gone.)' : '(The mirror keeps working on the other disk. That is the whole point of RAID 1. Check: cat /proc/mdstat)') + '\n'); } else if (has('--remove', '-r')) { if (!d || d.state !== 'faulty') { c.err(`mdadm: hot remove failed for /dev/${dev}: Device or resource busy\n` + C.dim('(you can only remove a disk marked faulty: --fail it first)') + '\n'); return 1; } r.devices.splice(r.devices.indexOf(d), 1); c.outln(`mdadm: hot removed /dev/${dev} from /dev/${name}`); } else { if (d) { c.err(`mdadm: /dev/${dev} is already in /dev/${name}\n`); return 1; } r.devices.push({ dev, state: 'active' }); r.syncStart = Date.now(); r.recovering = true; c.outln(`mdadm: added /dev/${dev}`); c.err(C.dim('(Rebuilding: the mirror copies everything onto the new disk. Watch with cat /proc/mdstat )') + '\n'); } c.vfs.changed(); return 0; } c.err('mdadm: unrecognised word or option. Try mdadm --help\n'); return 1; }, { only: ['linux'] }); def('mkfs mkfs.ext4 mke2fs mkfs.xfs', (c) => { const dev = c.args.find((x) => x.startsWith('/dev/')); if (!dev) { c.err(`Usage: ${c.name} /dev/DEVICE\n`); return 1; } if (!isRoot(c)) { c.err(`${c.name}: Permission denied while trying to determine filesystem size\n` + C.dim('(try sudo)') + '\n'); return 1; } const md = arrays(c.m)[devName(dev)]; const disk = c.m.disks.find((x) => x.dev === devName(dev)); if (!md && !(disk && disk.lab)) { c.err(`${c.name}: ${dev} contains a mounted filesystem or is not a practice disk\n` + C.dim('(TerminalEX only formats the RAID array /dev/md0 or the lab disks /dev/sdc, /dev/sdd.)') + '\n'); return 1; } const size = md ? blocks(md) * 1024 : disk.size; c.outln(`mke2fs 1.47.0 (5-Feb-2023)\nCreating filesystem with ${Math.round(size / 4096)} 4k blocks and ${Math.round(size / 16384)} inodes\nFilesystem UUID: ${TX.sha1(dev + Date.now()).slice(0, 8)}-${TX.sha1(dev).slice(8, 12)}-4c1e-9d55-${TX.sha1(dev).slice(12, 24)}\nSuperblock backups stored on blocks: \n\t32768, 98304, 163840, 229376, 294912, 819200, 884736, 1605632\n\nAllocating group tables: done \nWriting inode tables: done \nCreating journal (262144 blocks): done\nWriting superblocks and filesystem accounting information: done`); const lines = buf.split('\n'); const out = []; lines.forEach((l) => { out.push(l); const m = /^(sd[cd])\s/.exec(l); if (m && !c.args.includes('-f')) A.filter((a) => a.devices.some((d) => d.dev === m[1] && d.state !== 'faulty')).forEach((a, i) => out.push(`└─${a.name}`.padEnd(m[1].length + 5) + ` 9:${i} 0 ${human(blocks(a) * 1024).padStart(5)} 0 raid${a.level} ${a.mount || ''}`)); }); c.out(out.join('\n')); return r; } }); // ================================================================== Windows: diskpart and Storage Spaces const winPools = (m) => (m.extra.pools = m.extra.pools || []); const winVdisks = (m) => (m.extra.vdisks = m.extra.vdisks || []); TX.diskpartExtra = (c, line, sel) => { // returns true when handled. sel = { disk } const a = line.trim().toLowerCase().split(/\s+/); const d = sel.disk; const needDisk = () => { if (!d) { c.outln('There is no disk selected.\nPlease select a disk and try again.\n'); return true; } return false; }; const guard = () => { if (!d.lab) { c.outln(C.red(`DiskPart has refused to modify Disk ${d.num}.`) + '\n' + C.dim('(TerminalEX protects the system and data disks. Practice on the lab disks: Disk 3 and Disk 4.)') + '\n'); return true; } return false; }; if (a[0] === 'online' && a[1] === 'disk') { if (needDisk() || guard()) return true; d.offline = false; c.outln('DiskPart successfully onlined the selected disk.\n'); c.vfs.changed(); return true; } if (a[0] === 'offline' && a[1] === 'disk') { if (needDisk() || guard()) return true; d.offline = true; c.outln('DiskPart successfully offlined the selected disk.\n'); c.vfs.changed(); return true; } if (a[0] === 'attributes' && a[1] === 'disk') { if (needDisk()) return true; if (a[2] === 'clear' || a[2] === 'set') { if (guard()) return true; if (a[3] === 'readonly') d.readonly = a[2] === 'set'; c.outln(`Disk attributes ${a[2] === 'set' ? 'set' : 'cleared'} successfully.\n`); c.vfs.changed(); return true; } c.outln(`Current Read-only State : ${d.readonly ? 'Yes' : 'No'}\nRead-only : ${d.readonly ? 'Yes' : 'No'}\nBoot Disk : ${d.num === 0 ? 'Yes' : 'No'}\nPagefile Disk : ${d.num === 0 ? 'Yes' : 'No'}\nHibernation File Disk : ${d.num === 0 ? 'Yes' : 'No'}\nCrashdump Disk : ${d.num === 0 ? 'Yes' : 'No'}\nClustered Disk : No\n`); return true; } if (a[0] === 'convert' && ['gpt', 'mbr', 'dynamic', 'basic'].includes(a[1])) { if (needDisk() || guard()) return true; if (d.offline) { c.outln('Virtual Disk Service error:\nThe disk is offline.\n' + C.dim('(type: online disk)') + '\n'); return true; } if (a[1] === 'dynamic') d.dynamic = true; else if (a[1] === 'basic') d.dynamic = false; else d.style = a[1].toUpperCase(); c.outln(`DiskPart successfully converted the selected disk to ${a[1] === 'gpt' ? (x ? 1 : 0) : x); const isNum = (x) => typeof x === 'number' || isF(x) || typeof x === 'boolean'; const typeName = (x) => (x === null ? 'NoneType' : typeof x === 'boolean' ? 'bool' : typeof x === 'number' ? 'int' : isF(x) ? 'float' : typeof x === 'string' ? 'str' : Array.isArray(x) ? 'list' : x instanceof PyTuple ? 'tuple' : x instanceof Map ? 'dict' : x instanceof Set ? 'set' : x instanceof PyFunc || typeof x === 'function' ? 'function' : x && x.__module ? 'module' : 'object'); function fmtFloat(v) { if (!isFinite(v)) return isNaN(v) ? 'nan' : v > 0 ? 'inf' : '-inf'; if (Number.isInteger(v) && Math.abs(v) < 1e16) return v.toFixed(1); let s = String(v); if (/e/.test(s)) s = s.replace(/e\+?(-?)(\d)$/, 'e$10$2').replace('e+', 'e+'); return s; } function repr(x) { if (typeof x === 'string') return x.includes("'") && !x.includes('"') ? '"' + x + '"' : "'" + x.replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/\n/g, '\\n').replace(/\t/g, '\\t') + "'"; return str(x); } function str(x) { if (x === null || x === undefined) return 'None'; if (x === true) return 'True'; if (x === false) return 'False'; if (typeof x === 'number') return String(x); if (isF(x)) return fmtFloat(x.v); if (typeof x === 'string') return x; if (Array.isArray(x)) return '[' + x.map(repr).join(', ') + ']'; if (x instanceof PyTuple) return '(' + x.items.map(repr).join(', ') + (x.items.length === 1 ? ',' : '') + ')'; if (x instanceof Map) return '{' + [...x.entries()].map(([k, v]) => repr(k) + ': ' + repr(v)).join(', ') + '}'; if (x instanceof Set) return x.size ? '{' + [...x].map(repr).join(', ') + '}' : 'set()'; if (x instanceof PyFunc) return ``; if (typeof x === 'function') return ``; if (x && x.__module) return ``; return String(x); } function truthy(x) { if (x === null || x === undefined || x === false || x === 0 || x === '') return false; if (isF(x)) return x.v !== 0; if (Array.isArray(x)) return x.length > 0; if (x instanceof PyTuple) return x.items.length > 0; if (x instanceof Map || x instanceof Set) return x.size > 0; return true; } function iter(x) { if (typeof x === 'string') return [...x]; if (Array.isArray(x)) return x.slice(); if (x instanceof PyTuple) return x.items.slice(); if (x instanceof Map) return [...x.keys()]; if (x instanceof Set) return [...x]; if (x && x.__range) return x.__range(); throw new PyErr('TypeError', `'${typeName(x)}' object is not iterable`); } function eq(a, b) { if (isNum(a) && isNum(b)) return num(a) === num(b); if (Array.isArray(a) && Array.isArray(b)) return a.length === b.length && a.every((x, i) => eq(x, b[i])); if (a instanceof PyTuple && b instanceof PyTuple) return a.items.length === b.items.length && a.items.every((x, i) => eq(x, b.items[i])); return a === b; } function cmp(a, b) { if (isNum(a) && isNum(b)) return num(a) - num(b); if (typeof a === 'string' && typeof b === 'string') return a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0; if (Array.isArray(a) && Array.isArray(b)) { for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(a.length, b.length); i++) { const c = cmp(a[i], b[i]); if (c) return c; } return a.length - b.length; } if (a instanceof PyTuple && b instanceof PyTuple) return cmp(a.items, b.items); throw new PyErr('TypeError', `'<' not supported between instances of '${typeName(a)}' and '${typeName(b)}'`); } const mkNum = (v, float) => (float || !Number.isInteger(v) ? new PyFloat(v) : v); // ---------------- tokenizer function tokenize(src) { const lines = src.replace(/\r/g, '').split('\n'); const toks = []; const indents = [0]; let depth = 0; let lineNo = 0; let i = 0; const text = lines.join('\n') + '\n'; let atLineStart = true; while (i < text.length) { if (atLineStart && depth === 0) { let j = i, col = 0; while (text[j] === ' ' || text[j] === '\t') { col += text[j] === '\t' ? } fstring(s, line) { const parts = []; let i = 0, buf = ''; while (i < s.length) { if (s[i] === '{' && s[i + 1] === '{') { buf += '{'; i += 2; continue; } if (s[i] === '}' && s[i + 1] === '}') { buf += '}'; for (const x of items) { await this.tick(); await this.assign(s.target, x, env); try { await this.execBlock(s.body, env); } catch (e) { if (e === BREAK) return; if (e === CONT) continue; throw e; } } if (s.els) await this.execBlock(s.els, env); return; } case 'with': { const v = await this.eval(s.e, env); if (s.as) this.assignName(s.as, v, env); try { await this.execBlock(s.body, env); } finally { if (v && v.__file && v.close) v.close([]); } return; } case 'break': throw BREAK; case 'continue': throw CONT; case 'def': this.assignName(s.name, new PyFunc(s.name, s.params, s.body, env, this), env); return; case 'class': { const cls = { __class: s.name }; this.assignName(s.name, cls, env); return; } case 'return': throw new Ret(s.v ? await this.eval(s.v, env) : null); case 'global': if (env) { env.globalNames = env.globalNames || new Set(); s.names.forEach((n) => env.globalNames.add(n)); } return; case 'import': for (const [n, as] of s.names) this.assignName(as.split('.')[0], this.module(n.split('.')[0]), env); return; case 'from': { const mod = this.module(s.mod); for (const [n, as] of s.names) { if (n === '*') Object.keys(mod).filter((k) => !k.startsWith('__')).forEach((k) => this.assignName(k, mod[k], env)); else { if (!(n in mod)) throw new PyErr('ImportError', `cannot import name '${n}' from '${s.mod}'`); this.assignName(as, mod[n], env); } } return; } case 'raise': { const v = s.v ? await this.eval(s.v, env) : null; if (v && v.__exc) throw new PyErr(v.__exc, v.msg); throw new PyErr('RuntimeError', 'No active exception to reraise'); } case 'del': { const t = s.target; if (t.k === 'index') { const obj = await this.eval(t.obj, env), idx = await this.eval(t.idx, env);
