} } else if (t.k === 'name') { if (env) env.vars.delete(t.v); else this.globals.delete(t.v); } return; } case 'assert': if (!truthy(await this.eval(s.cond, env))) throw new PyErr('AssertionError', s.msg ? str(await this.eval(s.msg, env)) : ''); return; case 'try': { try { await this.execBlock(s.body, env); if (s.els) await this.execBlock(s.els, env); } catch (e) { if (!(e instanceof PyErr) || e.pyType === 'SystemExit' || e.pyType === 'KeyboardInterrupt') throw e; } if (match) { if (h.as) this.assignName(h.as, { __exc: e.pyType, msg: e.message, toString() { return e.message; } }, env); await this.execBlock(h.body, env); handled = true; break; } } if (!handled) throw e; } finally { if (s.fin) await this.execBlock(s.fin, env); } return; } } throw new PyErr('SyntaxError', 'unsupported statement'); } binop(op, a, b) { if (op === '+') { if (typeof a === 'string' && typeof b === 'string') return a + b; if (Array.isArray(a) && Array.isArray(b)) return a.concat(b); if (a instanceof PyTuple && b instanceof PyTuple) return new PyTuple(a.items.concat(b.items)); if (isNum(a) && isNum(b)) return mkNum(num(a) + num(b), isF(a) || isF(b)); throw new PyErr('TypeError', typeof a === 'string' ? `can only concatenate str (not "${typeName(b)}") to str` : `unsupported operand type(s) for +: '${typeName(a)}' and '${typeName(b)}'`); } if (op === '*') { if (typeof a === 'string' && typeof b === 'number') return a.repeat(Math.max(0, b)); if (typeof b === 'string' && typeof a === 'number') return b.repeat(Math.max(0, a)); if (Array.isArray(a) && typeof b === 'number') { let r = []; for (let i = 0; i < b; i++) r = r.concat(a); return r; } if (isNum(a) && isNum(b)) return mkNum(num(a) * num(b), isF(a) || isF(b)); throw new PyErr('TypeError', `can't multiply sequence by non-int of type '${typeName(b)}'`); } if (op === '%' && typeof a === 'string') { const vals = b instanceof PyTuple ? b.items : [b]; let k = 0; return a.replace(/%([-0-9.]*)([sdfr%])/g, (_, f, t) => (t === '%' ? '%' : t === 'd' ? String(Math.trunc(num(vals[k++]))) : t === 'f' ? num(vals[k++]).toFixed(f.includes('.') ? +f.split('.')[1] : 6) : t === 'r' ? repr(vals[k++]) : str(vals[k++]))); } if (!isNum(a) || !isNum(b)) throw new PyErr('TypeError', `unsupported operand type(s) for ${op}: '${typeName(a)}' and '${typeName(b)}'`); const x = num(a), y = num(b), f = isF(a) || isF(b); switch (op) { case '-': return mkNum(x - y, f); case '/': if (y === 0) throw new PyErr('ZeroDivisionError', 'division by zero'); return new PyFloat(x / y); case '//': if (y === 0) throw new PyErr('ZeroDivisionError', 'integer division or modulo by zero'); return mkNum(Math.floor(x / y), f); case '%': if (y === 0) throw new PyErr('ZeroDivisionError', 'integer modulo by zero'); return mkNum(((x % y) + y) % y, f); case '**': return mkNum(Math.pow(x, y), f || y < 0); case '&': return x & y; case '|': return x | y; case '^': return x ^ y; case '<<': return x << y; case '>>': return x >> y; } throw new PyErr('TypeError', 'bad operand'); } async eval(e, env) { switch (e.k) { case 'lit': return e.v; case 'name': return this.lookup(e.v, env, e.line); case 'concat': { let s = ''; for (const p of e.parts) s += p.k === 'fmt' ? await this.fmt(p, env) : str(await this.eval(p, env)); return s; } case 'list': { const out = []; for (const x of e.items) out.push(await this.eval(x, env)); return out; } case 'tuple': { const out = []; for (const x of e.items) out.push(await this.eval(x, env)); return new PyTuple(out); } case 'set': { const out = new Set(); for (const x of e.items) out.add(await this.eval(x, env)); return out; } case 'dict': { const m = new Map(); for (const [k, v] of e.pairs) m.set(this.key(await this.eval(k, env)), await this.eval(v, env)); return m; } case 'comp': { const out = e.kind === 'dict' ? new Map() : e.kind === 'set' ? new Set() : []; const cenv = { vars: new Map(), parent: env }; const rec = async (i) => { if (i === e.fors.length) { const v = await this.eval(e.el, cenv); if (e.kind === 'dict') out.set(this.key(v), await this.eval(e.valEl, cenv)); else if (e.kind === 'set') out.add(v); else out.push(v); return; } const f = e.fors[i]; for (const x of iter(await this.eval(f.it, cenv))) { await this.tick(); await this.assign(f.target, x, cenv); let ok = true; for (const c of f.conds) if (!truthy(await this.eval(c, cenv))) { ok = false; break; } if (ok) await rec(i + 1); } }; await rec(0); return out; } case 'ternary': return truthy(await this.eval(e.cond, env)) ? this.eval(e.a, env) : this.eval(e.b, env); case 'or': { const l = await this.eval(e.l, env); return truthy(l) ? l : this.eval(e.r, env); } case 'and': { const l = await this.eval(e.l, env); return truthy(l) ? this.eval(e.r, env) : l; } case 'not': return !truthy(await this.eval(e.e, env)); case 'unary': { const v = await this.eval(e.e, env); if (!isNum(v)) throw new PyErr('TypeError', `bad operand type for unary ${e.op}: '${typeName(v)}'`); return e.op === '-' ? mkNum(-num(v), isF(v)) : e.op === '~' ? ~num(v) : v; } case 'bin': return this.binop(e.op, await this.eval(e.l, env), await this.eval(e.r, env)); case 'cmp': { let l = await this.eval(e.first, env); for (let i = 0; i < e.ops.length; i++) { const r = await this.eval(e.rest[i], env); const op = e.ops[i]; let ok; switch (op) { case '==': ok = eq(l, r); break; case '!=': ok = !eq(l, r); break; case '<': ok = cmp(l, r) < 0; break; case '>': ok = cmp(l, r) > 0; break; case '<=': ok = cmp(l, r) <= 0; break; case '>=': ok = cmp(l, r) >= 0; break; case 'in': case 'not in': { let has; if (typeof r === 'string') has = r.includes(str(l)); else if (r instanceof Map) has = r.has(this.key(l)); else has = iter(r).some((x) => eq(x, l)); ok = op === 'in' ? has : !has; break; } case 'is': ok = l === r || (l === null && r === null); break; case 'is not': ok = !(l === r); break; } if (!ok) return false; l = r; } return true; } case 'index': { const obj = await this.eval(e.obj, env), idx = await this.eval(e.idx, env); if (typeof obj === 'string' || Array.isArray(obj) || obj instanceof PyTuple) { const arr = obj instanceof PyTuple ? repr(v) : null; return s !== null ? s : formatSpec(v, p.spec); } method(obj, name) { const I = this; const bind = (fn) => { fn.pyName = name; return fn; }; if (typeof obj === 'string') { const m = { upper: () => obj.toUpperCase(), lower: () => obj.toLowerCase(), title: () => obj.toLowerCase().replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase()), capitalize: () => obj.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + obj.slice(1).toLowerCase(), swapcase: () => [...obj].map((c) => (c === c.toUpperCase() ? obj.replace(new RegExp(`[${esc(a[0])}]+$`), '') : obj.replace(/\s+$/, '')), split: (a) => (a[0] === undefined || a[0] === null ? obj.trim().split(/\s+/).filter((x) => x !== '') : obj.split(str(a[0]))), splitlines: () => obj.split(/\r?\n/), join: (a) => iter(a[0]).map((x) => { if (typeof x !== 'string') throw new PyErr('TypeError', `sequence item 0: expected str instance, ${typeName(x)} found`); return x; }).join(obj), replace: (a) => obj.split(str(a[0])).join(str(a[1])), find: (a) => obj.indexOf(str(a[0])), index: (a) => { const i = obj.indexOf(str(a[0])); if (i < 0) throw new PyErr('ValueError', 'substring not found'); return i; }, count: (a) => obj.split(str(a[0])).length - 1, startswith: (a) => obj.startsWith(str(a[0])), endswith: (a) => obj.endsWith(str(a[0])), isdigit: () => /^\d+$/.test(obj), isalpha: () => /^[a-z]+$/i.test(obj), isalnum: () => /^[a-z0-9]+$/i.test(obj), isupper: () => /[A-Z]/.test(obj) && obj === obj.toUpperCase(), islower: () => /[a-z]/.test(obj) && obj === obj.toLowerCase(), isspace: () => /^\s+$/.test(obj), isnumeric: () => /^\d+$/.test(obj), format: (a, kw) => { let k = 0; return obj.replace(/\{([^{}]*)\}/g, (_, spec) => { const [key, fs] = spec.split(':'); const v = key === '' ? null : a[1]); return obj.get(k); }, copy: () => new Map(obj) }; if (m[name]) return bind(m[name]); } if (obj instanceof Set) { const m = { add: (a) => { obj.add(a[0]); return null; }, remove: (a) => { obj.delete(a[0]); return null; }, discard: (a) => { obj.delete(a[0]); return null; } }; if (m[name]) return bind(m[name]); } if (obj && obj.__file) { const f = obj[name]; if (f) return bind((a) => f(a)); } if (obj && typeof obj === 'object' && obj[name] !== undefined) return obj[name]; throw new PyErr('AttributeError', `'${typeName(obj)}' object has no attribute '${name}'`); } async callFn(f, args, kw, line) { if (typeof f === 'function') return f(args, kw); if (f && f.__pyType) return this.builtins[f.__pyType] ? this.builtins[f.__pyType](args, kw) : null; if (f && f.__class) { const o = { __cls: f.__class }; return o; } if (!(f instanceof PyFunc)) throw new PyErr('TypeError', `'${typeName(f)}' object is not callable`, line); const env = { vars: new Map(), parent: f.closure }; const pos = args.slice(); for (const p of f.params) { if (p.star === '*') { env.vars.set(p.name, new PyTuple(pos.splice(0))); continue; } if (p.star === '**') { const m = new Map(); Object.keys(kw).forEach((k) => m.set(k, kw[k])); env.vars.set(p.name, m); continue; } if (pos.length) env.vars.set(p.name, pos.shift()); else if (p.name in kw) env.vars.set(p.name, kw[p.name]); else if (p.def) env.vars.set(p.name, await this.eval(p.def, f.closure)); else throw new PyErr('TypeError', `${f.name}() missing 1 required positional argument: '${p.name}'`, line); } if (pos.length) throw new PyErr('TypeError', `${f.name}() takes ${f.params.length} positional argument${f.params.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} but ${args.length} were given`, line); this.depth = (this.depth || 0) + 1; if (this.depth > 400) { this.depth = 0; throw new PyErr('RecursionError', 'maximum recursion depth exceeded'); } try { await this.execBlock(f.body, env); return null; } catch (e) { if (e instanceof Ret) return e.v; throw e; } finally { this.depth--; } } } function esc(s) { return str(s).replace(/[\]\\^-]/g, '\\$&'); } function pyTrace(e, file, src) { const line = e.line || 1; const code = src ? (src.split('\n')[line - 1] || '').trim() : ''; if (e.pyType === 'SyntaxError' || e.pyType === 'IndentationError') return ` File "${file}", line ${line}\n ${code}\n ${'^'.repeat(Math.max(1, Math.min(code.length, 4)))}\n${e.pyType}: ${e.message}\n`; return `Traceback (most recent call last):\n File "${file}", line ${line}, in ${code && file !== '' ? '\n ' + code : ''}\n${e.pyType}${e.message ? ': ' + e.message : ''}\n`; } async function python(ctx, args) { const interp = new PyInterp(ctx); const files = args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-')); if (args.includes('--version') || args.includes('-V')) { ctx.out('Python 3.12.3\n'); return 0; } if (args[0] === '-c') { try { await interp.run(args.slice(1).join(' '), ''); return 0; } catch (e) { if (e instanceof PyErr) { ctx.err(pyTrace(e, '', args[1])); return 1; } throw e; } } if (files.length) { let src; try { src = ctx.vfs.readFile(ctx.resolve(files[0])); } catch (e) { ctx.err(`python3: can't open file '${ctx.fmt(ctx.resolve(files[0]))}': [Errno 2] No such file or directory\n`); return 2; } interp.argv = args.filter((a, i) => i >= args.indexOf(files[0])); const flush = () => (interp.openFiles || []).forEach((f) => f.close([])); try { await interp.run(src, files[0]); flush(); return 0; } catch (e) { if (e instanceof PyErr) { if (e.pyType === 'SystemExit') { (interp.openFiles || []).forEach((f) => f.close([])); return e.exitCode || 0; } if (e.pyType === 'KeyboardInterrupt') { ctx.err('Traceback (most recent call last):\n File "' + files[0] + '", line ' + (interp.line || 1) + '\nKeyboardInterrupt\n'); return 130; } ctx.err(pyTrace(e, files[0], src)); return 1; } if (e.interrupt) { ctx.err('\nKeyboardInterrupt\n'); return 130; } throw e; } } if (ctx.stdin != null) { try { await interp.run(ctx.stdin); } catch (e) { if (e instanceof PyErr) ctx.err(pyTrace(e, '', ctx.stdin)); } return 0; } ctx.out(`Python 3.12.3 (main, Sep 11 2024, 14:17:37) [GCC 13.2.0] on ${ctx.m.kind === 'windows' ? 'win32' : ctx.m.kind === 'mac' ? 'darwin' : 'linux'}\nType "help", "copyright", "credits" or "license" for more information.\n${C.dim('(TerminalEX mini-Python: great for practicing basics. Type exit() or press Ctrl+D to leave.)')}\n`); interp.interactive = true; let buf = []; while (true) { let line; try { line = await ctx.term.readLine({ prompt: buf.length ? '... ' : '>>> ', onEOF: true, history: ctx.pyHistory || (ctx.pyHistory = []) }); } catch (e) { return 0; } if (line === null) { ctx.out('KeyboardInterrupt\n'); buf = []; continue; } if (line === '\x04') return 0; if (line.trim()) (ctx.pyHistory || []).push(line); buf.push(line); const src = buf.join('\n'); const needMore = buf.length === 1 ? /:\s*$/.test(line) || /[([{]\s*$/.test(line) || /\\$/.test(line) : line.trim() !== ''; if (needMore) continue; buf = []; try { await interp.run(src + '\n'); } catch (e) { if (e instanceof PyErr) { if (e.pyType === 'SystemExit') return 0; ctx.err(pyTrace(e, '', src)); continue; } if (e.interrupt) { ctx.out('\nKeyboardInterrupt\n'); continue; } ctx.err('InternalError: ' + e.message + '\n'); } } } // ================================================================= Node.js function nodeInspect(v, depth = 0) { if (v === undefined) return C.gray('undefined'); if (v === null) return C.bold('null'); if (typeof v === 'string') return depth ? C.green("'" + v + "'") : C.green("'" + v + "'"); if (typeof v === 'number' || typeof v === 'bigint') return C.yellow(String(v) + (typeof v === 'bigint' ? 'n' : '')); if (typeof v === 'boolean') return C.yellow(String(v)); if (typeof v === 'function') return C.cyan(`[Function: ${v.name || '(anonymous)'}]`); if (typeof v === 'symbol') return C.green(v.toString()); if (Array.isArray(v)) { if (depth > 2) return C.cyan('[Array]'); return '[ ' + v.slice(0, 50).map((x) => nodeInspect(x, depth + 1)).join(', ') + (v.length > 50 ? `, ... ${v.length - 50} more items` : '') + ' ]'; } if (v instanceof Date) return C.magenta(v.toISOString()); if (v instanceof Map) return `Map(${v.size}) { ` + [...v.entries()].map(([k, x]) => nodeInspect(k, depth + 1) + ' => ' + nodeInspect(x, depth + 1)).join(', ') + ' }'; if (v instanceof Set) return `Set(${v.size}) { ` + [...v].map((x) => nodeInspect(x, depth + 1)).join(', ') + ' }'; if (v instanceof Error) return v.stack ? v.stack.split('\n').slice(0, 1).join('\n') : String(v); if (typeof v === 'object') { if (depth > 2) return C.cyan('[Object]'); const ks = Object.keys(v); if (!ks.length) return '{}'; return '{ ' + ks.slice(0, 30).map((k) => (/^[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*$/.test(k) ? k : "'" + k + "'") + ': ' + nodeInspect(v[k], depth + 1)).join(', ') + ' }'; } return String(v); } async function node(ctx, args) { if (args.includes('--version') || args.includes('-v')) { ctx.out('v18.19.1\n'); return 0; } const scope = Object.create(null); const out = (...a) => ctx.out(a.map((x) => (typeof x === 'string' ? x : TX.stripAnsi(nodeInspect(x)))).join(' ') + '\n'); const consoleObj = { log: out, info: out, warn: out, error: out, table: (x) => out(x), clear: () => ctx.term.clear() }; const sandbox = new Proxy(scope, { has: () => true, get: (t, k) => { if (k === Symbol.unscopables) return undefined; if (k === 'console') return consoleObj; if (k === 'require') return (m) => { if (m === 'fs') return { readFileSync: (p) => ctx.vfs.readFile(ctx.resolve(String(p))), writeFileSync: (p, d) => ctx.vfs.writeFile(ctx.resolve(String(p)), String(d), { owner: ctx.user }) }; if (m === 'os') return { platform: () => (ctx.m.kind === 'windows' ? 'win32' : ctx.m.kind === 'mac' ? 'darwin' : 'linux'), hostname: () => ctx.m.net.hostname, cpus: () => Array(ctx.m.hw.threads).fill({ model: ctx.m.hw.cpu }) }; throw new Error(`Cannot find module '${m}'`); }; if (k === 'process') return { argv: ['node'].concat(args), platform: ctx.m.kind === 'windows' ? 'win32' : ctx.m.kind === 'mac' ? 'darwin' : 'linux', version: 'v18.19.1', exit: () => { throw new TX.ExitShell(0); } }; if (k in t) return t[k]; if (k in window && ['Math', 'JSON', 'Date', 'Array', 'Object', 'String', 'Number', 'parseInt', 'parseFloat', 'isNaN', 'Map', 'Set', 'Promise', 'setTimeout', 'Symbol', 'RegExp', 'Error', 'Boolean', 'BigInt', 'Infinity', 'NaN', 'undefined', 'encodeURIComponent', 'decodeURIComponent'].includes(k)) return window[k]; if (typeof k === 'string') throw new ReferenceError(`${k} is not defined`); return undefined; }, set: (t, k, v) => { t[k] = v; return true; }, }); // eslint-disable-next-line no-new-func const runner = new Function('sandbox', 'code', 'with (sandbox) { return eval(code); }'); const prep = (code) => code.replace(/^\s*(?:let|const|var)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)\s*=/gm, '$1 =').replace(/^\s*function\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)\s*\(/gm, '$1 = function $1(').replace(/^\s*class\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)/gm, '$1 = class $1'); const files = args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-')); if (files.length) { let src; try { src = ctx.vfs.readFile(ctx.resolve(files[0])); } catch (e) { ctx.err(`node:internal/modules/cjs/loader:1148\n throw err;\n ^\n\nError: Cannot find module '${ctx.fmt(ctx.resolve(files[0]))}'\n at Module._resolveFilename (node:internal/modules/cjs/loader:1145:15)\n\nNode.js v18.19.1\n`); return 1; } try { runner(sandbox, prep(src)); return 0; } catch (e) { if (e.exitShell) return 0; ctx.err(`${ctx.fmt(ctx.resolve(files[0]))}:1\n\n${e.name}: ${e.message}\n\nNode.js v18.19.1\n`); return 1; } } ctx.out(`Welcome to Node.js v18.19.1.\nType ".help" for more information.\n`); while (true) { let line; try { line = await ctx.term.readLine({ prompt: '> ', onEOF: true, history: ctx.nodeHistory || (ctx.nodeHistory = []), highlight: null }); } catch (e) { return 0; } if (line === null) { ctx.out('(To exit, press Ctrl+C again or Ctrl+D or type .exit)\n'); continue; } if (line === '\x04' || line.trim() === '.exit') return 0; if (line.trim() === '.help') { ctx.out('.break Sometimes you get stuck, this gets you out\n.clear Alias for .break\n.editor Enter editor mode\n.exit Exit the REPL\n.help Print this help message\n'); continue; } if (!line.trim()) continue; ctx.nodeHistory.push(line); try { const v = runner(sandbox, prep(line)); ctx.out(nodeInspect(v) + '\n'); } catch (e) { if (e.exitShell) return 0; ctx.out(`Uncaught ${e.name}: ${e.message}\n`); } } } TX.langs = { python, node }; // register as POSIX commands const posixCtx = (c) => ({ out: c.out, err: c.err, term: c.term, vfs: c.vfs, m: c.m, user: c.user, tty: c.tty, stdin: c.stdin, signal: c.signal, resolve: (p) => c.sh.resolve(p), fmt: (s) => c.sh.fmt(s), cwd: c.sh.cwd, readLine: c.readLine }); TX.defPosix('python3 python', (c) => python(posixCtx(c), c.args), { pkg: 'python3' }); TX.defPosix('node nodejs', (c) => node(posixCtx(c), c.args), { pkg: 'node' }); })();
